中文摘要：代理式AI系统中的故障可能源于多个大语言模型（LLM）代理之间交换消息的交互。逐点归因无法区分联合必要的修复与替代性的单事件修复。我们提出了最小修复族恢复（MRFR）：恢复所有包含最小的事件集，这些事件集的反事实重放能在声明的规模界限内恢复任务成功。我们提出了图约束联合重放（GCJR），它从执行依赖图中切分与故障相关的事件，构造图可行的单事件和成对候选，并通过与配对干净对应物的重放来验证它们。对于固定的重放结果，GCJR在其声明的图域内是精确的。在来自120-DAG受控基准的90个范围内案例中，GCJR实现了1.000的族精确匹配，同时将平均重放调用从56.3减少到25.3（55.1%），相对于穷举搜索。在24案例、四代理LLM试点中，它再次实现了1.000的族精确匹配，并将平均模型调用从21.0减少到10.0（52.4%）；单事件重放会遗漏联合必要的修复。

Original abstract: Failures in agentic AI systems can arise from interactions among messages exchanged by multiple large language model (LLM) agents. Pointwise attribution cannot distinguish a jointly necessary repair from alternative singleton repairs. We formulate Minimal Repair Family Recovery (MRFR): recovering all inclusion-minimal event sets whose counterfactual replay restores task success within a declared size bound. We propose Graph-Constrained Joint Replay (GCJR), which slices failure-relevant events from an execution dependency graph, constructs graph-feasible singleton and pair candidates, and verifies them by replay with paired clean counterparts. For fixed replay outcomes, GCJR is exact within its declared graph domain. On 90 in-scope cases from a 120-DAG controlled benchmark, GCJR achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match while reducing mean replay calls from 56.3 to 25.3 (55.1%) relative to exhaustive search. On a 24-case, four-agent LLM pilot, it again achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match and reduces mean model calls from 21.0 to 10.0 (52.4%); single-event replay misses jointly necessary repairs.

</details>


### [31] [GuardianAgent: Policy-Conditioned Risk-Adaptive Anonymization with Verified Adversarial Escalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29251)
*Ruiyi Yang,Gayathri Lihinikaduarachchi,Rahat Masood,Flora D. Salim,Salil S. Kanhere*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 策略条件风险自适应与验证性对抗升级的Agent框架，具有可迁移的机制设计。

TL;DR: GuardianAgent是一个策略条件匿名化框架，通过显式风险评分和验证的自适应重写，在实时网络流量中实现隐私保护，并在多个基准上取得最佳隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护系统仅检测私有片段，而缺少对目标站点隐私策略的合规性判断以及基于残余风险的精细化重写，导致过度匿名化或隐私泄露。

Method: GuardianAgent使用AMRSF风险评分公式结合多个因素来决定允许/转换/拒绝及匿名化级别；采用证据快速路径与LLM慢速路径结合；并使用验证对抗性猜测器驱动的五级重写层次，避免幻觉造成的过匿名化。

Result: 在TAB、SynthPAI和PII-Masking-300k三个基准上，GuardianAgent实现最强隐私-效用权衡，唯一在所有三个领域隐私得分超过0.90，并在骨干切换下保持鲁棒；压力测试显示不同上下文会调整决策和匿名化强度。

Conclusion: GuardianAgent验证了显式风险建模和验证性重写对策略条件匿名化的有效性，为代理系统在动态网络环境中的隐私保护提供了可靠方案。

中文摘要：实时网络流量的隐私保护不仅仅需要检测私有片段。基于代理的隐私保护系统必须确定外发操作是否符合目标网站的隐私政策，然后仅应用根据残余披露风险合理化的重写或清理级别。我们提出了GuardianAgent，一个策略条件的匿名化框架，将结构化风险评估与验证的自适应重写相结合。GuardianAgent通过AMRSF（自适应多因素风险评分公式）计算风险，这是一个显式控制器，将违反政策可能性与数据敏感性、接收者传输、目的合法性、上下文依据和政策透明度相结合，而不是依赖LLM直接分配风险。该风险评分决定允许/转换/拒绝决策以及初始匿名化级别。为了提高效率，GuardianAgent使用证据快速路径处理低不确定性的策略匹配，仅在不确定情况下调用LLM慢速路径。对于重写，它采用由验证的对抗性猜测器驱动的五级层次结构：仅在原始文本支持猜测时触发升级，防止幻觉的攻击者置信度导致不必要的过度匿名化。跨三个基准的实验，涵盖法律文本（TAB）、Reddit帖子（SynthPAI）和多格式合成PII记录（PII-Masking-300k），表明GuardianAgent在已发布的基线中实现了最强的隐私-效用权衡，并且是唯一在所有三个领域达到超过0.90隐私的方法，在骨干网络切换下保持鲁棒。动作上下文压力测试进一步表明，相同的外发文本在不同的接收者、目的、行动依据和政策透明度条件下会收到不同的决策和匿名化强度。

Original abstract: Privacy protection for live web traffic requires more than detecting private spans. Agent-based privacy protection systems must determine whether an outgoing action complies with the destination site's privacy policy, then apply only the level of rewriting or sanitisation justified by the residual disclosure risk. We present GuardianAgent, a policy-conditioned anonymization framework that couples structured risk assessment with verified adaptive rewriting. GuardianAgent computes risk through AMRSF (Adaptive Multi-factor Risk Scoring Formula), an explicit controller that combines policy-violation likelihood with data sensitivity, recipient transmission, purpose legitimacy, contextual basis, and policy transparency, rather than relying on an LLM to assign risk directly. This risk score determines both the allow/transform/deny decision and the initial anonymization level. For efficiency, GuardianAgent uses an evidential fast path for low-uncertainty policy matches and invokes an LLM slow path only for uncertain cases. For rewriting, it applies a five-level hierarchy driven by a verified adversarial guesser: guesses trigger escalation only when supported by the original text, preventing hallucinated attacker confidence from causing unnecessary over-anonymization. Experiments across three benchmarks spanning legal text (TAB), Reddit posts (SynthPAI), and multi-format synthetic PII records (PII-Masking-300k) show that GuardianAgent achieves the strongest privacy-utility trade-off among published baselines and is the only method to reach more than 0.90 privacy in all three domains, remaining robust under a backbone switch. Action-context stress tests further show that the same outgoing text receives different decisions and anonymization strengths under different recipients, purposes, action bases, and policy-transparency conditions.

</details>


### [32] [Dynamic Important Example Mining for Reinforcement Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29252)
*Haoru Tan,Sitong Wu,Yanfeng Chen,Shizhen Zhao,Yang-Tian Sun,Tianjia Liu,Chirui Chang,Shaofeng Zhang,Samm Sun,Xiuzhe Wu,Ruobing Xie,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

Journal reference: CVPR-2026

Selection reason: 强化微调中的动态重要样本挖掘，可迁移到Agent训练的数据选择。

TL;DR: 提出一种动态重要样本挖掘框架DIEM，通过梯度对齐重要性估计和约束批重加权，使强化微调中的数据利用自适应，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心的RFT方法多采用静态或启发式样本选择，假设样本价值固定，忽略了策略学习的非平稳性，导致次优更新。

Method: DIEM在每个优化步骤中集成两个组件：梯度对齐重要性估计器近似样本边际贡献，以及约束批重加权方案在保持梯度幅度稳定的同时最大化总效用。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，DIEM一致优于强静态和动态基线。

Conclusion: DIEM证明了自适应数据利用在RFT中的有效性，为强化微调提供了一种更优的数据选择与加权策略，代码已开源。

中文摘要：强化微调（RFT）越来越多地被用于增强大型模型的推理能力，然而其有效性受限于训练数据的选择和使用方式。大多数以数据为中心的RFT方法依赖于静态或启发式的样本选择，隐含地假设样本的价值在训练过程中是固定的。这忽略了策略学习的非平稳动态，可能导致次优的更新。我们提出了动态重要样本挖掘（DIEM），一个原则性的、全自动的框架，使数据利用在RFT过程中自适应。DIEM在每个优化步骤中整合两个组件：（i）梯度对齐重要性估计器，高效近似每个样本对策略改进的边际贡献；（ii）约束批重加权方案，在保持更新的梯度幅度以稳定优化的同时最大化总效用。在多个推理基准上，DIEM一致优于强静态和动态基线。代码将通过https://github.com/hrtan/DIEM发布。

Original abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) is increasingly used to strengthen the reasoning abilities of large models, yet its effectiveness is bound by how training data are selected and used. Most data-centric RFT methods rely on static or heuristic sample selection, implicitly assuming a sample's value is fixed over training. This overlooks the non-stationary dynamics of policy learning and can lead to suboptimal updates. We propose Dynamic Important Example Mining (DIEM), a principled and fully automated framework that makes data utilization adaptive throughout RFT. DIEM integrates two components into each optimization step: (i) a gradient-alignment importance estimator that efficiently approximates each sample's marginal contribution to policy improvement; and (ii) a constrained batch reweighting scheme that maximizes aggregate utility while preserving the update's gradient magnitude to stabilize optimization. Across several reasoning benchmarks, DIEM consistently outperforms strong static and dynamic baselines. The code will be released via https://github.com/hrtan/DIEM.

</details>


### [33] [RACER: Reinforced Agent Collaboration for Explainable Reasoning on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29263)
*Yuwei Lou,Hao Hu,Yuzhou Jiang,Zongfei Zhang,Liang Wang,Jincai Liu,Jidong Ge,Xianping Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 1 figures, This paper has been accepted by ICONIP 2026

Selection reason: 强化多Agent协作与共享记忆的KG推理，多角色协作机制对Coding Agent有直接启发。

TL;DR: RACER是一个基于强化学习和多智能体协作的框架，通过语义感知剪枝、共享记忆图和多路径精炼，显著提升了知识图谱增强LLM在复杂推理任务上的表现和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG增强LLM方法依赖单智能体路径提取和固定提示，面对大规模KG时搜索空间巨大且缺乏适应性，同时单路径生成容易导致推理不完整或错误。

Method: RACER采用语义感知动作剪枝和教师引导强化学习提取高质量路径；引入跨任务累积共享记忆图和注意力驱动的多路径知识精炼模块；通过四个智能体（GraphAgent、TemplateAgent、AnswerAgent、CriticAgent）协作动态优化提示并评估答案。

Result: 在CommonsenseQA和OpenBookQA数据集上，RACER相较于最先进的KG增强LLM基线平均提升5%，并提供稳健且高度可解释的推理。

Conclusion: RACER通过多智能体协作和强化学习机制有效缓解了LLM幻觉和复杂推理问题，显著超越现有方法，展示了在知识图增强推理中的强大潜力。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）经常遭受幻觉问题，并在需要多跳领域知识的复杂推理任务上表现困难。虽然集成知识图谱（KG）提供了结构化且可验证的信息来源，但当前KG增强的LLM范式通常依赖单智能体路径提取和固定提示，缺乏适应性并面临巨大的搜索空间。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了RACER，一个用于知识图谱上可解释推理的强化智能体协作框架。RACER采用语义感知的动作剪枝和教师引导的强化学习机制，从大规模知识图谱中高效提取高质量推理路径。此外，为缓解单路径生成的缺陷，我们引入了一个跨任务累积的共享记忆图，并配以注意力驱动的多路径知识精炼模块。最后，RACER通过一个四角色多智能体协作系统（GraphAgent、TemplateAgent、AnswerAgent和CriticAgent）编排这些组件，以动态优化提示并评估答案。在CommonsenseQA和OpenBookQA数据集上的大量实验表明，RACER显著优于最先进的KG增强LLM基线，平均提升5%，提供了强大且高度可解释的推理能力。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucination and struggle with complex reasoning tasks requiring multi-hop domain knowledge. While integrating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) provides a structured and verifiable information source, current KG-enhanced LLM paradigms usually rely on single-agent path extraction and fixed prompting, lacking adaptability and facing huge search spaces. To address these challenges, we propose RACER, a Reinforced Agent Collaboration framework for Explainable Reasoning on knowledge graphs. RACER employs a semantic-aware action pruning and teacher-guided reinforcement learning mechanism to efficiently extract high-quality reasoning pathways from large-scale KGs. Furthermore, to mitigate single-path generation pitfalls, we introduce a cross-task accumulated shared memory graph paired with an attention-driven multi-path knowledge refinement module. Finally, RACER orchestrates these components through a four-role multi-agent collaboration system (GraphAgent, TemplateAgent, AnswerAgent, and CriticAgent) to dynamically refine prompts and evaluate answers. Extensive experiments on CommonsenseQA and OpenBookQA datasets demonstrate that RACER significantly outperforms state-of-the-art KG-enhanced LLM baselines with an average improvement of 5\%, offering robust and highly interpretable reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [34] [MMPCBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models on Proactive Critique of Flawed Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29286)
*Jinzhe Li,Gengxu Li,Jinnan Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出MLLM主动批判错误输入的评测框架，其错误检测与诊断能力对agent输入验证和可靠性设计有潜在启发。

TL;DR: 提出了MMPCBench，一个评估多模态大语言模型主动批评能力的综合框架，发现主流模型在主动批评上存在明显弱点，并揭示“一致性差距”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估忽略了对错误用户输入的主动处理，只关注理想情况或简单拒绝行为，无法全面衡量MLLMs的可靠性，因此需要专门的评估框架。

Method: 构建MMPCBench，包含4种主要错误类型和12个子类别的细粒度分类；采用分层评估协议测量错误检测、诊断和解决性能；使用对齐感知指标评估内部推理与最终响应的一致性。

Result: 测试14个主流MLLMs发现其在主动批评方面明显不足，尤其对微妙视觉异常处理较弱；存在普遍的一致性差距，即推理模型内部能正确识别错误但最终输出中抑制这些判断以服从指令。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs的主动批评能力亟待提升，MMPCBench为相关研究和改进提供了评估基准，并揭示了需要关注内部推理与输出对齐的问题。

中文摘要：随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）演变为复杂的交互式助手，其可靠性不仅取决于遵循指令，还取决于验证指令。我们将“主动批评”（Proactive Critique）定义为模型无需额外提示即可自主识别、分析和修复错误用户输入的能力。然而，现有评估主要在理想环境或简单拒绝行为下测试模型，很大程度上忽略了主动错误处理。为填补这一空白，我们提出了 MMPCBench，一个用于评估 MLLMs 主动批评能力的综合框架。它包含一个细粒度的分类体系，涵盖 4 种主要错误类型、12 个子类别，从跨模态矛盾到缺失视觉前提。我们采用分层评估协议来衡量模型的错误检测、诊断和解决性能，并应用对齐感知指标来评估内部推理与最终响应之间的一致性。对 14 个主流 MLLMs 的测试显示，它们在主动批评方面存在明显弱点，尤其是在处理微妙视觉异常时。值得注意的是，我们发现了普遍存在的“一致性差距”：推理模型通常能在内部推理中正确识别和分析错误，但为优先保证响应合规性，在最终输出中抑制了这些有效见解。代码和数据可在 https://github.com/ALIENS32/MMPCBench 获取。

Original abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) evolve into sophisticated interactive assistants, their reliability depends not only on following instructions but also on validating them. We define Proactive Critique as the model's autonomous ability to identify, analyze and fix faulty user inputs without extra prompts. However, evaluations mainly test models under ideal circumstances or simple refusal behaviors, largely ignoring active error processing. To fill this gap, we propose MMPCBench, a comprehensive framework for evaluating MLLMs' proactive critique competence. It features a fine-grained taxonomy of 4 primary error types spanning 12 subcategories, ranging from cross-modal contradictions to missing visual premises. We adopt a hierarchical evaluation protocol to measure models' error detection, diagnosis and resolution performance, and apply alignment-aware metrics to assess the coherence between internal reasoning and final responses. Tests on 14 mainstream MLLMs show obvious weaknesses in proactive critique, especially in dealing with subtle visual anomalies. Notably, we identify a pervasive "consistency gap": reasoning models can often correctly identify and analyze errors during internal reasoning yet suppress these valid insights in final outputs to prioritize response compliance. The code and data is available at https://github.com/ALIENS32/MMPCBench.

</details>


### [35] [BIRD-History: A Benchmark for History-Driven Text-to-SQL with Fine-Grained Knowledge Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29345)
*Yunfan Zhou,Qiming Shi,Yizhou Yang,Di Weng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: EMNLP, 2026

Selection reason: 历史SQL日志检索驱动的text-to-SQL方法，可类比coding agent中经验/轨迹复用，提升欠限定需求处理。

TL;DR: 提出了BIRD-History基准，包含1,393个任务，用于评估文本到SQL系统利用历史SQL日志处理隐式领域知识查询的能力；同时提出一种即插即用检索器，从历史脚本中提取并检索相关知识，在四个系统上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based文本到SQL系统在处理依赖领域特定知识（如业务逻辑、数据约定）的模糊查询时表现不佳，而历史SQL查询日志蕴含这类知识，但缺乏相应基准来评估历史驱动方法。

Method: 构建BIRD-History基准（11个数据库、1,393个任务），标注历史查询中相关知识与SQL子句的对应关系；提出即插即用检索器，提取五类外部知识，检索并重排序相关片段，用于增强查询生成，且无需修改提示。

Result: 在四个文本到SQL系统上实验均取得一致性能提升，验证了利用历史查询日志处理未充分指定查询的有效性。

Conclusion: BIRD-History基准和所提检索器填补了历史驱动文本到SQL评估的空白，展示了历史日志在增强领域知识利用方面的价值；数据集和代码已开源。

中文摘要：尽管基于大型语言模型（LLM）的文本到SQL系统在标准基准测试上取得了令人瞩目的性能，但当用户查询隐式依赖领域特定知识（例如业务逻辑、数据约定和分析实践）时，这些系统会表现不佳，而此类知识既未在模式中捕获，也未在自然语言问题中明确说明。历史SQL查询日志提供了此类知识的宝贵来源，然而现有基准测试不足以支持对历史驱动方法的评估。为解决这一空白，我们引入了BIRD-History，一个由11个数据库中的1,393个任务组成的基准测试，旨在评估文本到SQL系统利用历史SQL脚本为未充分指定的自然语言问题提供依据的能力。每个任务都标注了真实标签，指明哪些历史查询包含相关知识以及哪些SQL子句对其进行了编码，从而能够系统评估检索效果和知识利用情况。除基准测试外，我们提出了一种即插即用的检索器，该检索器从历史SQL脚本中提取五种类型的外部知识，然后检索并重新排序相关片段以用于查询生成。该检索器无缝集成到现有的少样本文本到SQL流程中，无需修改提示。实验表明，四个文本到SQL系统均获得了一致的改进，凸显了利用历史查询日志处理未充分指定查询的价值。数据集和代码已在https://github.com/zjuidg/BIRD-History开源。

Original abstract: While recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based text-to-SQL systems achieve impressive performance on standard benchmarks, they struggle when user queries implicitly rely on domain-specific knowledge, such as business logic, data conventions, and analytical practices, that is neither captured by the schema nor explicitly stated in the natural language question. Historical SQL query logs offer a valuable source of such knowledge, yet existing benchmarks do not adequately support evaluation of history-driven approaches. To address this gap, we introduce BIRD-History, a benchmark consisting of 1,393 tasks across 11 databases, designed to evaluate text-to-SQL systems' ability to ground underspecified natural language questions using historical SQL scripts. Each task is annotated with ground-truth labels specifying which historical queries contain relevant knowledge and which SQL clauses encode it, enabling systematic evaluation of both retrieval effectiveness and knowledge utilization. Alongside the benchmark, we propose a plug-in retriever that extracts five types of external knowledge from historical SQL scripts, then retrieves and reranks relevant fragments for query generation. The retriever integrates seamlessly into existing few-shot text-to-SQL pipelines without requiring prompt modifications. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across four text-to-SQL systems, highlighting the value of leveraging historical query logs for handling underspecified queries. Dataset and code are open-sourced on https://github.com/zjuidg/BIRD-History.

</details>


### [36] [LiteSearch-VL: Small Multimodal Search Agents via Trajectory Distillation and Synthetic Step-DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29357)
*Saeed Khaki,Nima Safaei,Kamal Ginotra*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过轨迹蒸馏和合成步骤DPO训练小型多模态搜索agent，其工具调用与偏好学习方法可迁移到coding agent紧凑化训练。

TL;DR: 将大型多模态搜索智能体的轨迹蒸馏到小型 Qwen3-VL 模型时，主要迁移的是行为契约而非均匀准确率；2B 模型经全轨迹 SFT 后达到 28.4% Pass@1，超过 4B 基线，而合成偏好与工具蒸馏仅带来小幅改进，且答案验证成为下一瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强多模态搜索智能体依赖昂贵的前沿模型或大型开放模型，本文研究在单节点预算下将已发布智能体轨迹蒸馏到更小骨干时实际迁移了什么，以探索低成本路线。

Method: 提出 LiteSearch-VL 低计算配方，基于 Qwen3-VL-2B/4B，仅用 OpenSearch-VL 轨迹、LoRA 适配器，并用 GPT-5 生成的硬负样本进行 DPO 合成步骤级偏好，覆盖五种局部失败模式；并进行全轨迹 SFT、偏好学习、工具蒸馏与步骤预算消融。

Result: 在 12,400 个 GPT-5 评判 rollout 上，全轨迹 SFT 使 2B 模型从 1,237/1,240 无答案变为 28.4% Pass@1，超过 4B 基线 25.6%；最佳 4B 配置为 30.8%；额外搜索轮次主要将弃权转为 wrong_entity 错误而非正确答案。

Conclusion: 行为迁移是大模型到小模型蒸馏的主导效应，小模型获得智能体契约；合成的偏好与工具蒸馏只是精细改进；答案验证而非搜索深度是小规模多模态智能体的下一个关键瓶颈。

中文摘要：多模态搜索智能体通过交错进行图像理解、网络检索、工具使用和证据综合来回答视觉问题。现有强大系统存在于两种昂贵模式：专有前沿模型（如 GPT-5 和 Gemini），或经过大量智能体数据和强化学习训练的大型开放视觉-语言骨干模型。我们提出一个不同的问题：当已发布的智能体轨迹在单节点预算下被蒸馏到更小的骨干模型中时，实际迁移了什么？我们通过 LiteSearch-VL 对此进行研究，这是一种针对 Qwen3-VL-2B 和 Qwen3-VL-4B 的低计算量方案，仅使用已发布的 OpenSearch-VL 轨迹、参数高效的 LoRA 适配器以及合成步骤级偏好：在针对五种局部失败模式（过早回答、错误工具、弱查询、重复查询、忽略图像）的 GPT-5 生成的硬负样本上进行 DPO。在 SimpleVQA、FVQA、LiveVQA 和 VDR-Bench-testmini 上由 GPT-5 评判的 12,400 次 rollout 中，主导效应是行为性的而非均匀的准确率提升：全轨迹监督微调迁移了智能体契约，使 2B 模型从几乎从不发出可用答案（1,237/1,240 次无答案 rollout）变为 28.4% 的宏观 Pass@1，匹配或略超开箱即用的 4B 基础模型（25.6%）。合成偏好学习和紧凑工具蒸馏起到改进而非相位转变的作用（最佳 4B 配置：30.8% 宏观 Pass@1）。最后，受控的 VDR 步骤预算消融显示，额外的搜索轮次将弃权转化为错误实体错误而非正确答案，从而指出答案验证（而非搜索深度）是小规模多模态智能体的下一个瓶颈。

Original abstract: Multimodal search agents answer visual questions by interleaving image understanding, web retrieval, tool use, and evidence synthesis. Strong systems exist, but in two expensive regimes: proprietary frontier models such as GPT-5 and Gemini, or large open vision-language backbones trained with substantial agentic data and reinforcement learning. We ask a different question: when released agent trajectories are distilled into much smaller backbones under a single-node budget, what is actually transferred? We study this with LiteSearch-VL, a low-compute recipe for Qwen3-VL-2B and Qwen3-VL-4B that uses only released OpenSearch-VL trajectories, parameter-efficient LoRA adapters, and synthetic step-level preferences: DPO on GPT-5-generated hard negatives targeting five local failure modes (premature answer, wrong tool, weak query, repeated query, ignored image). Across 12,400 GPT-5-judged rollouts on SimpleVQA, FVQA, LiveVQA, and VDR-Bench-testmini, the dominant effect is behavioral rather than a uniform accuracy lift: full-trajectory supervised fine-tuning transfers the agent contract, taking the 2B model from almost never emitting a usable answer (1,237/1,240 no_answer rollouts) to 28.4% macro Pass@1, matching or slightly exceeding the off-the-shelf 4B base (25.6%). Synthetic preference learning and compact tool distillation act as refinements rather than phase transitions (best 4B configuration: 30.8% macro Pass@1). Finally, a controlled VDR step-budget ablation shows that extra search turns convert abstentions into wrong_entity errors rather than correct answers, identifying answer verification, not search depth, as the next bottleneck for small multimodal agents.

</details>


### [37] [TRACER: Per-Tool Context Retention for LLM Agents via Consequence-Attributed Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29363)
*Ziqi Lin,Ye Wu,Mengying Yang,Xu Liu,Yizhou Liu,Qiang Ke,Qin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出后果感知的每工具上下文保留策略，用强化学习平衡token消耗与任务成功，对长程agent上下文管理有通用价值。

TL;DR: TRACER通过后果感知的逐工具保留策略，在保持任务成功率的同时，显著减少长视野语言代理的上下文令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩策略未考虑删除工具输出的下游后果，激进压缩可能导致昂贵的工具重新调用，抵消节省，形成压缩-后果差距。

Method: TRACER将压缩建模为顺序逐工具决策问题，使用轻量级REINFORCE策略分配查询条件保留比率，并通过学习的结果模型改进信用分配，目标函数联合优化任务成功、令牌消耗和重新调用成本。

Result: 在三个压缩器后端上，TRACER减少总令牌消耗29-46%并保持或提升任务成功率；相比静态策略额外节省15-18%；跨架构迁移节省18-25%。

Conclusion: 后果感知的逐工具上下文保留能有效提升长视野语言代理的效率，验证了考虑压缩后果的重要性。

中文摘要：企业数据代理通过在多步推理过程中链接许多工具调用来回答业务查询，每个会话通常会积累数十万个上下文令牌。现有的压缩策略通常在不考虑删除单个工具输出对下游后果的情况下分配保留预算。因此，激进的压缩可能会触发昂贵的工具重新调用，从而抵消初始节省。我们将这称为压缩-后果差距。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了TRACER，它将压缩表述为一个顺序的逐工具决策问题。一个轻量级的REINFORCE策略仅使用每次压缩事件时可用的信息来分配查询条件下的保留比率。其后果感知目标共同考虑任务成功率、总令牌消耗和压缩后的工具重新调用。为了改进信用分配，TRACER使用一个学习到的结果模型，将所选保留比率的预测后果与完全保留每个工具输出的后果进行比较。在三个压缩器后端的保留生产查询上，TRACER相对于保留所有上下文，将总令牌消耗减少了29-46%，同时保持相当或更高的任务成功率。与工具类型条件的静态策略相比，TRACER额外提供了15-18%的令牌节省。干预性推出表明，学习到的逐工具信用分数与测量的单工具后果相关。学习到的策略在跨代理主干和压缩器架构迁移时也产生了正向节省，并在五个保留的LOCA-bench环境上减少了18-25%的令牌消耗。这些结果证明了后果感知的、逐工具上下文保留对于提高长视野语言代理效率的价值。

Original abstract: Enterprise data agents answer business queries by chaining many tool calls over multiple reasoning steps, routinely accumulating hundreds of thousands of context tokens per session. Existing compression strategies typically allocate retention budgets without accounting for the downstream consequences of removing individual tool outputs. Aggressive compression may therefore trigger costly tool re-invocations that offset the initial savings. We call this the compression--consequence gap. To close it, we propose TRACER, which formulates compression as a sequential per-tool decision problem. A lightweight REINFORCE policy assigns query-conditioned retention ratios using only information available at each compression event. Its consequence-aware objective jointly accounts for task success, total token consumption, and post-compression tool re-invocations. To improve credit assignment, TRACER uses a learned outcome model to compare the predicted consequences of the selected retention ratio with those of fully retaining each tool output. On held-out production queries across three compressor backends, TRACER reduces total token consumption by 29--46% relative to keeping all context while maintaining comparable or higher task success. Compared with a tool-type-conditional static policy, TRACER provides an additional 15--18% of token savings. Interventional rollouts show that the learned per-tool credit scores correlate with measured single-tool consequences. The learned policy also yields positive savings when transferred across agent backbones and compressor architectures, and reduces token consumption by 18--25% on five held-out LOCA-bench environments. These results demonstrate the value of consequence-aware, per-tool context retention for improving the efficiency of long-horizon language agents.

</details>

*Xiangxin Luo,Chengtian Hong,Haohua Li,Yongyi Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: FORESIGHT-9 不仅评估组合结果，更检验自适应智能体状态与执行在替代未来中的连贯性；发布世界线、轨迹、审计追踪与重生成脚本，为可审计的自适应交易评估提供新标准。

中文摘要：回溯式回测对自适应交易智能体提供的检验有限：它们无法排除历史污染，无法揭示对单一已实现市场路径的敏感性，也无法揭示长期自适应过程中的内部退化。我们提出了 FORESIGHT-9，一个前瞻性的、关注过程的基准，它由九条可审计的反事实压力世界线构成，这些世界线从共同的 2026 年 7 月信息边界分支而出。每条世界线规定了分阶段的宏观金融事件和联合多资产锚点；一个确定性生成器实现轨迹，而观测则按照世界内时间披露。一个通用契约标准化了观测和执行，同时保留每个智能体原生的自适应循环。我们使用两个基础模型主干评估了两个自适应交易智能体框架，共进行了 36 次长期运行。智能体排名在不同世界线和主干之间差异很大，而固定的等权策略在 36 次运行中优于 31 次。过程遥测揭示了最终收益所掩盖的失败：在一次高收益运行中，实时因子库崩溃，而实际执行的持仓收敛到等权回退，尽管决策记录继续报告活跃的因子集成。因此，FORESIGHT-9 不仅评估投资组合结果，还评估自适应智能体状态和执行在不同替代未来中是否保持一致。我们发布了世界线、轨迹、审计跟踪和再生成脚本。

Original abstract: Retrospective backtests provide a limited test of adaptive trading agents: they cannot rule out historical contamination, expose sensitivity to a single realized market path, or reveal internal degeneration during long-horizon adaptation. We introduce FORESIGHT-9, a prospective and process-aware benchmark built from nine auditable counterfactual stress worldlines branching from a common July 2026 information boundary. Each worldline specifies staged macro-financial events and joint multi-asset anchors; a deterministic generator realizes the trajectories, while observations are disclosed according to in-world time. A common contract standardizes observations and execution while preserving each agent's native adaptation loop. We evaluate two adaptive trading-agent frameworks with two foundation-model backbones across 36 long-horizon runs. Agent rankings vary substantially across worldlines and backbones, and a fixed equal-weight policy outperforms 31 of 36 runs. Process telemetry exposes failures that terminal returns conceal: in one high-return run, the live factor library collapsed while executed holdings converged to the equal-weight fallback, even though decision records continued to report an active factor ensemble. FORESIGHT-9 therefore evaluates not only portfolio outcomes, but whether adaptive agent state and execution remain coherent across alternative futures. We release the worldlines, trajectories, audit traces, and regeneration scripts.

</details>


### [39] [Can escalation channels redirect reward hacking toward defect disclosure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29460)
*Francesca Gomez*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages

Selection reason: 研究coding agent在缺陷测试上的reward hacking并通过升级渠道引导上报，与自动代码优化agent的可靠性和安全控制直接相关。

TL;DR: 通过为编码智能体提供升级渠道，将奖励黑客行为大幅降低，并提升缺陷检测能力，且无性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在遇到有缺陷的测试基础设施时可能进行奖励黑客行为，甚至已在现实入侵中出现；需要一种既能减少此类行为又能揭示底层缺陷的干预方法。

Method: 采用2×2析因设计，在8个前沿模型上测试升级工具、独立反奖励黑客策略及其组合，评估对奖励黑客和缺陷检测的影响。

Result: 组合干预将奖励黑客从23.6%降至5.3%（OR=9.2），对6/8模型完全消除；升级与黑客互斥，缺陷检测覆盖率提升10.1个百分点，触发时准确率99.4%对85.8%。

Conclusion: 升级渠道是有效且无开销的干预，可将智能体能力导向披露而非利用，优于遏制性方法，并可作为诊断工具。

中文摘要：当编码智能体遇到有缺陷的测试基础设施时，它们可能会进行奖励黑客（reward hacking）：硬编码输出或修改测试文件以通过它们无法合法满足的测试。这种模式现在已出现在基准测试之外，发生在一场针对某主要AI平台生产基础设施的协调多智能体入侵中。让智能体能够检测并利用缺陷的相同能力，在正确的决策环境下，也可能让其报告缺陷。我们评估了升级渠道（escalation channels），即智能体在冲突点可用的结构化报告工具，作为一种决策环境干预，既能减少奖励黑客，又能揭示触发它的基础设施缺陷。一个2×2析因设计区分了升级工具、独立的反奖励黑客策略及其组合的贡献。在涵盖5个家族的8个前沿模型中，组合干预将奖励黑客从23.6%降至5.3%（混合效应逻辑回归OR=9.2，95% CI 5.0–16.8，p<10^-12），且没有可检测的成本或性能开销，并对8个模型中的6个完全消除了奖励黑客。升级与黑客行为近乎完美地互斥，96.8%的升级不涉及黑客行为。除了减少黑客行为，升级渠道还充当诊断基础设施：在监控的基础上，升级额外增加了10.1个百分点的缺陷检测覆盖率，并且一旦触发就更准确（99.4%对85.8%）。与可能因能力增长而面临失控风险的基于遏制的方法不同，升级渠道将能力重定向到披露而非利用。

Original abstract: When coding agents encounter defective test infrastructure they may reward-hack: hardcoding outputs or editing test files to pass tests they cannot legitimately satisfy, a pattern that has now appeared outside benchmarks, in a coordinated multi-agent intrusion of a major AI platform's production infrastructure. The same capability that lets an agent detect and exploit a defect could let it report one, given the right decision environment. We evaluate escalation channels, structured reporting tools available to the agent at the point of conflict, as a decision-environment intervention that both reduces reward hacking and surfaces the infrastructure defects that trigger it. A $2 \times 2$ factorial separates the contributions of an escalation tool, a standalone anti-reward-hacking policy, and their combination. Across 8 frontier models spanning 5 families, the combined intervention reduces reward hacking from 23.6\% to 5.3\% (mixed-effects logistic OR = 9.2, 95\% CI 5.0--16.8, $p < 10^{-12}$) with no detectable cost or performance overhead, eliminating it entirely for 6 of 8 models. Escalation and hacking are near-perfectly mutually exclusive, with 96.8\% of escalations involving no hacking. Beyond reduction, escalation channels function as diagnostic infrastructure: on top of monitoring, escalation adds +10.1 percentage points of defect detection coverage and is more accurate once it fires (99.4\% vs 85.8\%). Unlike containment-based approaches that risk outpacing growing model capabilities, escalation channels redirect capability toward disclosure rather than exploitation.

</details>


### [40] [Call Neighbours Yourself: Graph Walks with Destination-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29588)
*Yilun Liu,Boyu Luo,Yanran Tang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: 让LLM通过图上行走动作主动探索邻居并用自蒸馏解决延迟奖惩，其探索-利用训练机制可迁移到agent的工具选择与信息获取。

TL;DR: CNY框架让LLM在推理过程中自主选择邻居，通过拓扑约束的图游走和目的地条件的策略内自蒸馏，在文本属性图推理上优于固定上下文的基线，并具备良好的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成前固定邻居集合，导致推理局限于静态上下文，无法在推理过程中获取缺失证据，无法适应需要动态证据的推理任务。

Method: 提出CNY框架，让LLM通过拓扑约束的图游走动作主动探索邻居；引入目的地条件的策略内自蒸馏，根据邻居内容揭示后的动作偏好变化生成训练信号，解决探索延迟信用问题。

Result: 在标准TAG推理基准上的统一原始文本设置下，CNY一致优于固定上下文的后期训练基线；学习到的探索策略能迁移到未见图和未遇到的图级任务。

Conclusion: 邻居选择应作为推理的一部分，将探索动态融入推理过程可显著提升LLM在文本属性图上的推理能力，并具有良好的泛化性。

中文摘要：对文本属性图（TAGs）进行推理要求大型语言模型（LLMs）将节点的文本与分布在其邻域中的证据结合起来。现有方法在生成之前固定可访问的邻居集合，迫使推理在静态上下文上运行，并阻止模型在推理过程中获取缺失的证据。我们认为邻居选择本身应该是推理过程的一部分。为此，我们提出了“自己召唤邻居”（CNY）框架，该框架使LLMs能够通过拓扑约束的图游走操作主动探索图邻域。CNY不是在一个预选的邻居集合上进行推理，而是暴露轻量级的邻居预览，并学习何时扩展候选邻居以获取额外证据。为了解决邻居探索的延迟信用挑战，我们引入了目的地条件的策略内自蒸馏，它在所选邻居的内容被揭示后对其进行回顾性评估，并将由此产生的动作偏好变化转换为动作级别的训练信号。在统一原始文本设置下的标准TAG推理基准上的实验表明，CNY始终优于固定上下文的后期训练基线。此外，学习到的探索策略可以迁移到未见过的图和训练期间未遇到的图级任务。代码可在 https://github.com/superallen13/CNY 获取。

Original abstract: Reasoning over text-attributed graphs (TAGs) requires large language models (LLMs) to combine a node's text with evidence distributed across its neighbourhood. Existing methods fix the set of accessible neighbours before generation, forcing reasoning to operate over a static context and preventing the model from acquiring missing evidence during inference. We argue that neighbour selection should itself be part of the reasoning process. To this end, we propose Call Neighbours Yourself (CNY), a framework that enables LLMs to proactively explore graph neighbourhoods through topology-constrained graph-walk actions. Instead of reasoning over a pre-selected neighbour set, CNY exposes lightweight neighbour previews and learns when to expand candidate neighbours for additional evidence. To address the delayed-credit challenge of neighbour exploration, we introduce destination-conditioned on-policy self-distillation, which retrospectively evaluates a selected neighbour after its content is revealed and converts the resulting change in action preference into an action-level training signal. Experiments on standard TAG reasoning benchmarks under a unified raw-text setting show that CNY consistently outperforms fixed-context post-training baselines. Furthermore, the learned exploration policy transfers to unseen graphs and to a graph-level task not encountered during training. Code is available at https://github.com/superallen13/CNY.

</details>


### [41] [Towards a Systems Foundation for Agentic Skills: Architecture, Lifecycle, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29596)
*Sanket Badhe,Deep Shah,Priyanka Tiwari,Nehal Kathrotia*

Main category: cs.AI

DOI: [10.2139/ssrn.7245419](https://doi.org/10.2139/ssrn.7245419)

Selection reason: 系统化定义agentic skills的架构、生命周期与安全治理，为可复用、可验证的编码agent技能设计提供参考框架。

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能体技能（agentic skills）的统一系统基础与参考架构，将其定义为外部化程序性知识，并通过九阶段生命周期系统化地构建了可扩展、健壮且可验证的自主语言智能体范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主大语言模型智能体在复杂长周期任务中面临可靠性、上下文消耗和执行稳定性瓶颈，而传统单体提示工程和无状态工具调用范式难以扩展，因此需要一种模块化、可重用且可移植的技能抽象来外部化执行知识。

Method: 将技能形式化为连接认知规划与确定性执行的外部化程序性知识，提出涵盖九个阶段（自主发现、编写与表示、记忆存储、检索与路由、组合与编排、执行与修复、终身适应、评估、安全治理）的参考架构，并分析市场动态、公共注册表、对抗性威胁及防御机制，最后对跨领域的系统实现进行分类。

Result: 建立了智能体技能生态系统的统一基础架构，系统化描述了其生命周期与安全机制，并在软件工程、操作系统导航、具身机器人和科学发现等领域进行了实现分类，同时指出了持续学习和基准现实性等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 智能体技能是构建可扩展、健壮且可验证的自主语言智能体的基础范式，为克服现有LLM智能体的瓶颈提供了系统化路径，但需进一步解决终身学习与基准真实性问题。

中文摘要：自主大语言模型（LLM）智能体在部署于复杂、长周期任务时，日益面临可靠性、上下文消耗和执行稳定性瓶颈。虽然单体提示工程和无状态工具调用范式难以扩展，该领域正迅速向“智能体技能”（agentic skills）收敛：即模块化的程序性抽象，将执行知识外部化为可重用、可执行且可移植的工件。本文为智能体技能生态系统建立了统一的系统基础和参考架构。我们将技能形式化为外部化的程序性知识，连接高层认知规划与确定性执行环境，并系统地描绘了跨越九个阶段生命周期的架构：自主发现、编写与表示格式、记忆存储、动态检索与路由、组合与编排、执行与修复、终身适应、实证评估以及安全治理。我们进一步考察了市场动态、公共注册表和新出现的对抗性威胁向量，以及运行时验证和防御机制。最后，我们跨软件工程、操作系统导航、具身机器人和科学发现对系统实现进行了分类，同时强调了持续学习和基准现实性方面的关键开放挑战。这项工作将智能体技能确立为构建可扩展、健壮且可验证的自主语言智能体的基础范式。

Original abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face reliability, context consumption, and execution stability bottlenecks when deployed on complex, long-horizon tasks. While monolithic prompt engineering and stateless tool-calling paradigms struggle to scale, the field is rapidly converging toward \emph{agentic skills}: modular procedural abstractions that externalize execution knowledge into reusable, executable, and portable artifacts. This paper establishes a unified systems foundation and reference architecture for the agentic skills ecosystem. We formalize skills as externalized procedural knowledge bridging high-level cognitive planning with deterministic execution environments, and systematically delineate the architecture across a nine-stage lifecycle: autonomous discovery, authoring and representation formats, memory storage, dynamic retrieval and routing, composition and orchestration, execution and repair, lifelong adaptation, empirical evaluation, and security governance. We further examine marketplace dynamics, public registries, and emerging adversarial threat vectors, alongside runtime verification and defense mechanisms. Finally, we categorize system implementations across software engineering, operating system navigation, embodied robotics, and scientific discovery, while highlighting critical open challenges in continual learning and benchmark realism. This work establishes agentic skills as a foundational paradigm for building scalable, robust, and verifiable autonomous language agents.

</details>


### [42] [LLMs Interpret, Embeddings Organize, Graphs Emerge: Agent-Driven Compilation of Scientific Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29612)
*Shi-Ju Ran,Kun Zhang,Xi Wu,Liu-Si Yang,Wen-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 (main text) + 6 (SM) pages, 4 + 1 figures

Selection reason: 构建知识编译管道时采用LLM生成与确定性校验相结合的agent设计，可作为agent harness中验证机制的参考。

TL;DR: 提出ASKS系统，通过LLM生成Wiki视图和机器语义，经确定性检查和图规则更新持久知识状态，实现可溯源的科学知识编译；在56篇论文上的实验验证了其稳定性和可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续科学工作需要跨任务共享解释并保留证据链，现有方法缺乏显式的、可审计的知识编译与溯源机制。

Method: ASKS系统：为每个来源用LLM生成Wiki视图和机器语义，通过确定性检查构建GraphDelta，结合嵌入几何和图规则更新持久状态，使每次摄取成为可检查的状态转换。

Result: 在56篇论文上的时间顺序编译中，分支存活率、跨论文支持、谱系、覆盖率和变动指标呈现稳定且低变动的Hub组织，规范节点主要以增添性增长，所有图级测量和导航路径均保留来源记录链接。

Conclusion: ASKS能够有效实现科学知识编译，生成可溯源的研究画像，并保持知识库的稳定性和可解释性，适用于长期科学工作流。

中文摘要：持续的科学工作需要一种知识基底，它能够跨任务承载解释，并保留通往来源证据的路径。我们将这一过程称为“科学知识编译”，并在ASKS（代理驱动的科学知识系统）中加以实现。对于每个来源，一个LLM生成可读的Wiki视图和面向机器的语义。确定性检查将后者转换为文档局部的GraphDelta，嵌入几何与显式图规则相结合，将提议的变更整合到持久状态中。每次摄取都是一次可检查的状态转换，作用于累积的知识，编译后的Wiki和图视图链接到保留的来源记录。我们通过按时间顺序编译来自同一研究项目的56篇已发表论文来检验这一过程。分支存活率、跨论文支持、谱系、覆盖率和变动产生了一个以张量网络方法为核心的、可溯源至作者的研究画像，其分支延伸至量子多体研究、张量网络机器学习和量子人工智能方向。在此次运行中，更高级别的Hub组织保持稳定且低变动。规范节点的增长主要是增添性的。图级测量和导航路径保留了指向其编译来源记录的链接。

Original abstract: Sustained scientific work requires a knowledge substrate that carries interpretation across tasks and preserves paths to source evidence. We call this process \emph{scientific knowledge compilation} and implement it in ASKS, the \emph{Agent-Driven Scientific Knowledge System}. For each source, an LLM produces a readable Wiki view and machine-facing semantics. Deterministic checks convert the latter into a document-local GraphDelta, and embedding geometry together with explicit graph rules integrates the proposed changes into persistent state. Each ingest is an inspectable state transition over accumulated knowledge, with compiled Wiki and graph views linked to the preserved source record. We examine this process by chronologically compiling 56 published papers from one research program. Branch survival, cross-paper support, lineage, coverage, and churn yield a source-traceable author research portrait centered on tensor-network methods, with branches into quantum many-body research, tensor-network machine learning, and quantum-AI-oriented directions. In this run, higher-level Hub organization remains stable and low-churn. Canonical-node growth is predominantly additive. Graph-level measurements and navigation paths retain links to the source records from which they were compiled.

</details>


### [43] [Detect Before You Attribute: Cascade Failure Attribution for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29646)
*Jiayi Zhang,Zexin Wang,Degang Sun,Changhua Pei,Fei Sun,Gaogang Xie,Jingjing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures, 13 tables

Selection reason: 提出多agent系统级联故障归因的检测过滤方法，显著提升归因准确率，直接服务于agent可靠性与调试。

TL;DR: DUOTRACE是一种即插即用的检测过滤器，通过先检测异常执行再提供聚焦证据，显著提升了LLM智能体失败归因的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体失败归因方法存在不足：拓扑/频谱方法忽略细粒度语义，LLM方法在长轨迹上上下文退化。需要一种能结合语义与结构并缓解长上下文问题的方法。

Method: 提出DUOTRACE，遵循检测-归因范式：利用VAE进行异常检测，集成双视图语义-结构节点表示、Tree-LSTM轨迹编码器，以及前缀链和LLM数据增强来处理异构节点、层次结构和有限失败数据。

Result: 在六个LLM归因基线上，DUOTRACE使智能体级和步骤级归因准确率分别提高8.7%和7.0%。

Conclusion: DUOTRACE通过先检测后归因的策略，有效提升了失败归因性能，为LLM智能体可靠性提供了一种即插即用的改进方案。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型（LLM）的智能体在通过多步推理解决复杂任务方面显示出强大的潜力，但仍然容易受到执行失败的影响。因此，准确的失败归因对于提高智能体可靠性至关重要。现有的基于拓扑和基于频谱的方法利用轨迹结构，但往往忽略细粒度语义，而基于LLM的归因方法能够捕捉语义线索，但在长轨迹上遭受长上下文退化问题。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了DUOTRACE，一种用于LLM失败归因的即插即用检测过滤器。DUOTRACE遵循“先检测后归因”范式：它首先检测异常执行，然后向下游基于LLM的归因方法提供聚焦的轨迹证据。为了在智能体轨迹上进行有效的基于VAE的异常检测，DUOTRACE集成了双视图语义-结构节点表示、基于Tree-LSTM的轨迹编码器，以及基于前缀链和LLM的数据增强，以处理异构节点、层次执行结构和有限的失败数据。使用六个基于LLM的归因基线进行的实验表明，DUOTRACE在智能体级和步骤级归因准确率上分别提高了8.7%和7.0%。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown strong potential in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning, yet they remain vulnerable to execution failures. Accurate failure attribution is therefore critical for improving agent reliability. Existing topology- and spectrum-based methods exploit trajectory structures but often overlook fine-grained semantics, while LLM-based attribution methods capture semantic cues but suffer from long-context degradation over lengthy trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose DUOTRACE, a plug-and-play detection filter for LLM-based failure attribution. DUOTRACE follows a detect-before-attribute paradigm: it first detects anomalous executions and then supplies focused trajectory evidence to downstream LLM-based attribution methods. For effective VAE-based anomaly detection on agent trajectories, DUOTRACE integrates dual-view semantic-structural node representations, a Tree-LSTM-based trajectory encoder, and prefix-chain- and LLM-based data augmentation to handle heterogeneous nodes, hierarchical execution structures, and limited failure data. Experiments with six LLM-based attribution baselines show that DUOTRACE improves agent-level and step-level attribution accuracy by 8.7% and 7.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [44] [Ideation Arena: Evaluating LLM Generated Research Ideas with Battle-style Human Expert Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29696)
*Zhiyu Chen,Keyu Zhao,Jigao Fu,Dong Liang,Yanbiao Wu,Jiaoyang Li,Haidong Xue,Xinhua Zeng,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用专家成对比拼评估LLM研究agent的构思质量，构建leaderboard与自动评估基准，对agent能力评测有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出了 Ideation Arena，一个通过成对人工评估来评价 LLM 研究想法质量的战斗式平台，并构建了 Elo 排行榜和自动评估基准，发现当前 LLM 评判员无法可靠复现专家偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究想法评估困难，缺乏客观标准和参考答案，且现有自动评估方法无法对齐人类偏好。

Method: 构建战斗式平台 Ideation Arena，使用共享文献上下文，让 14 个 LLM 和 5 个智能体架构生成想法，由 105 位研究者进行双盲成对比较，计算 Elo 评分并构建排行榜；同时构建 Ideation Arena Eval 基准，测试 LLM 评判员与人类偏好的一致性。

Result: 排行榜在标注者组成和领域覆盖变化下保持稳定；智能体效果差异大，部分提升构思质量，部分无益甚至更差；最佳 LLM 评判员在总体质量上的软准确率为 72.56%。

Conclusion: Ideation Arena 能有效评估研究想法，但当前自动评估器仍无法替代专家偏好，需要进一步改进。

中文摘要：评估由大型语言模型（LLM）产生的研究想法具有挑战性，因为其科学价值无法完全通过客观标准确定，且不存在单一参考答案来定义何为好想法。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了 Ideation Arena，一个战斗式平台，通过成对人工评估来评价研究想法。Ideation Arena 评估了由 14 个前沿 LLM 和基于 2 个基础模型构建的 5 种研究智能体架构所生成的想法。为确保共同的起点，Ideation Arena 从参与研究人员熟悉的论文中构建共享文献上下文，并将相同的上下文提供给所有 LLM 和智能体。我们收集了来自 105 位活跃计算机科学研究人员的超过 6,000 次双盲成对比较，并在共享封闭上下文协议下构建了计算机科学领域提案阶段专家偏好的 Elo 评分排行榜。我们通过评估者间一致性和稳健性分析验证了排名，表明排行榜在标注者组成和领域覆盖范围变化时保持稳定。我们的结果显示了智能体有效性的显著差异，一些框架相较于其基础模型提升了构思质量，而其他框架则几乎没有带来益处，甚至表现不如其基础模型。我们进一步构建了 Ideation Arena Eval，一个用于评估自动评估器在研究构思中是否与人类偏好一致的基准。使用当前 LLM 评判员的实验表明，它们仍不能可靠地重现专家偏好，最佳评判员在总体质量上的软准确率仅为 72.56%。我们的代码、数据和排行榜可在 https://github.com/foss12138/Research-Ideation-Arena 获取。

Original abstract: Evaluating research ideas generated by LLMs is difficult because their scientific value cannot be fully determined by objective criteria, and no single reference answer specifies what counts as a good idea. To address this challenge, we introduce Ideation Arena, a battle style platform that evaluates research ideas through pairwise human assessment. Ideation Arena evaluates ideas generated by 14 frontier LLMs and 5 research agent architectures built on 2 base models. To ensure a common starting point, Ideation Arena builds shared literature contexts from papers familiar to the participating researchers and provides the same contexts to all LLMs and agents. We collect over 6,000 double blind pairwise comparisons from 105 active computer science researchers and construct an Elo rating leaderboard of proposal-stage expert preferences in computer science under a shared closed-context protocol. We validate the rankings through interrater agreement and robustness analyses, showing that the leaderboard remains stable under changes in annotator composition and domain coverage. Our results show substantial variation in agent effectiveness, with some frameworks improving ideation quality over their backbones and others offering little benefit or even underperforming their base models. We further construct Ideation Arena Eval, a benchmark for assessing whether automated evaluators align with human preferences in research ideation. Experiments with current LLM judges show that they still cannot reliably reproduce expert preferences, with the best judge reaching 72.56% Soft Accuracy on Overall Quality. Our code, data, and leaderboards are available at https://github.com/foss12138/Research-Ideation-Arena.

</details>


### [45] [FRAMEWORKERS: A Dynamic Multi-Agent Framework for AI-Generated Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29814)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Letian Shi,Deheng Zhang,Ruibo Ming,Mengshun Hu,Dannong Xu,Jian Wang,Danda Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 动态多agent编排框架，以任务栈和工作区驱动，结合SFT与GRPO实现可靠路由和失败恢复，架构可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: FRAMEWORKERS是一个以任务为中心的多智能体视频制作框架，通过动态任务栈和共享工作区实现灵活编排，结合SFT和GRPO优化路由，显著提升视频质量、任务覆盖和故障恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成系统多为刚性流水线，难以适应多样输入和动态工作流；通用LLM在长周期编排和多模态资产路由上不可靠。需要一种能动态管理任务并可靠协调多步骤的框架。

Method: 提出FRAMEWORKERS多智能体框架，核心有Director（动态维护任务栈，决定下一子任务和子智能体）和Assistant（基于共享工作区执行任务，检索上下文并持久化成果）。子智能体通过描述符注册，便于扩展；Director通过SFT和GRPO进行描述符条件路由训练。

Result: 实验显示FRAMEWORKERS在路由准确率上优于强LLM规划器，能可靠恢复运行时故障，无需再训练即可泛化到未见过的子智能体，并在端到端视频质量和任务覆盖范围上超过固定流水线、单智能体系统和先前多智能体方法。

Conclusion: FRAMEWORKERS证明了任务中心化、工作区落地的多智能体编排方式能有效提升开放式视频生产的灵活性和可靠性，为长周期创作任务提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

中文摘要：现代视频生成器擅长合成单个片段，但完整的视频制作需要协调一系列相互依赖的创造性步骤，包括脚本编写、故事板制作、生成和编辑。它还要求随着中间输出、依赖关系和执行状态随时间演变，进行持久的资产管理和动态任务编排。现有的自动化系统通常依赖于刚性流水线，难以适应多样化的输入和变化的工作流，而通用大语言模型（LLM）在长周期编排和多模态资产路由方面仍然不可靠。我们引入了FRAMEWORKERS，一个以任务为中心、以工作区为基础的多智能体框架，用于开放式视频制作。中央导演（Director）将视频创建表述为动态任务管理，持续编辑任务栈（Task Stack）以决定接下来执行哪个子任务以及调用哪个子智能体。助手（Assistant）作为执行层，将每个选定任务基于共享工作区（Workspace）进行落地，检索所需资产和上下文，调用指定的子智能体，并持久化生成的成果。执行能力通过带有注册描述符的模块化子智能体暴露，使得新子智能体可以集成而无需重新设计编排工作流。为了提高编排可靠性，我们通过监督微调（SFT）和随后的组相对策略优化（GRPO）对导演进行微调，以进行描述符条件下的任务路由。实验表明，FRAMEWORKERS在路由准确性上优于强大的LLM规划器，能从运行时故障中可靠恢复，无需重新训练即可泛化到未见过的子智能体，并在端到端视频质量和任务覆盖范围上优于固定流水线、单智能体系统和先前的多智能体方法。

Original abstract: Modern video generators excel at synthesizing individual clips, but complete video production requires coordinating a long sequence of interdependent creative steps, including scripting, storyboarding, generation, and editing. It further demands persistent asset management and dynamic task orchestration as intermediate outputs, dependencies, and execution states evolve over time. Existing automated systems typically rely on rigid pipelines that are difficult to adapt to diverse inputs and changing workflows, while general-purpose large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for long-horizon orchestration and multimodal asset routing. We introduce FRAMEWORKERS, a task-centric and workspace-grounded multi-agent framework for open-ended video production. A central Director formulates video creation as dynamic task management, continuously editing a Task Stack to determine which subtask to execute next and which sub-agent to invoke. An Assistant serves as the execution layer, grounding each selected task in a shared Workspace, retrieving the required assets and context, invoking the assigned sub-agent, and persisting the resulting artifacts. Execution capabilities are exposed through modular sub-agents with registered descriptors, allowing new sub-agents to be integrated without redesigning the orchestration workflow. To improve orchestration reliability, we fine-tune the Director via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for descriptor-conditioned task routing. Experiments show that FRAMEWORKERS outperforms strong LLM planners in routing accuracy, recovers reliably from runtime failures, generalizes to unseen sub-agents without retraining, and achieves higher end-to-end video quality and broader task coverage than fixed pipelines, single-agent systems, and prior multi-agent approaches.

</details>


### [46] [Perceive to Hypothesize, Verify to Ground: An Agentic Reasoning Framework for Open-World Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29880)
*Yutian Jiang,Ruijie Li,Sisuo Lyu,Xixuan Hao,Qingxiang Liu,Yongzi Yu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出感知-假设-验证的agentic推理框架，强调证据锚定与验证，该模式可迁移到代码agent的事实核查与结果验证。

TL;DR: GeoPAVE提出一种双层智能体框架，将开放世界地理定位重构为感知-验证问题，并引入带推理轨迹的新数据集PAVED。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理定位方法依赖大视觉语言模型，但缺乏显式证据接地验证，导致感知幻觉和上下文漂移，难以处理模糊视觉线索。

Method: GeoPAVE采用双层智能体框架：第一层通过单次rollout进行感知驱动的假设生成；第二层通过验证驱动的证据接地来执行支持、反驳或细化决策动作。

Result: 引入了源自真实用户签到数据的新数据集PAVED，包含多跳查询、多轮工具调用和结构化感知-验证痕迹，并公开代码与数据。

Conclusion: 将开放世界地理定位视为类人的感知-验证问题，GeoPAVE配合PAVED数据集为证据接地的地理定位推理提供了新范式。

中文摘要：开放世界地理定位要求模型通过多步推理和外部知识接地来处理模糊的视觉线索。尽管近期的大型视觉语言模型展现出强大的多模态推理能力，但现有方法仍因缺乏显式的证据接地验证而遭受感知幻觉和上下文漂移。在这项工作中，我们将地理定位重新表述为一种类人的“感知-然后-验证”推理问题，并提出GeoPAVE（地理定位感知与验证引擎），这是一个双层智能体框架，包含基于感知的假设生成（通过单次rollout）和基于验证的证据接地，用于决策行动：支持、反驳和细化。为支持严格评估，我们进一步引入PAVED，这是一个源自真实用户签到数据的新数据集，配备全面的推理轨迹，具有多跳查询、多轮工具调用和结构化的感知-验证痕迹。数据集和代码可在https://github.com/Arandinglv/GeoPAVE获取。

Original abstract: Open-world geo-localization requires models to reason over ambiguous visual cues through multi-step reasoning and external knowledge grounding. While recent large vision-language models exhibit strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, existing approaches still suffer from perceptual hallucination and context drift due to the lack of explicit evidence-grounded verification. In this work, we reformulate geo-localization as a human-like perceive-then-verify reasoning problem and propose GeoPAVE (Geo-localization Perception-and-Verification-Engine), a bi-level agentic framework that contains perception-based hypothesis generation via single-pass rollouts and verification-based evidence grounding for decision actions: support, refute, and refine. To support rigorous evaluation, we further introduce PAVED, a novel dataset derived from real-world user check-in data, equipped with comprehensive reasoning trajectories featuring multi-hop queries, multi-round tool invocations, and structured perception-verification traces. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Arandinglv/GeoPAVE.

</details>


### [47] [SearchWiki: Learning to Build and Navigate Knowledge Wikis for Active Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29953)
*Guransh Singh,Vishwajeet Kumar,Arkadeep Acharya,Adnan Qidwai,Jaydeep Sen,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 训练agent在结构化wiki上多轮工具检索，用强化学习优化导航策略，是通用的信息获取agent方法。

TL;DR: 提出SearchWiki框架，将语料库构建为层次化维基，并用强化学习训练9B代理WikiResearcher-9B进行多步检索，在多个基准上超越平面检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面检索增强生成忽略文档层级和跨文档结构，导致检索效率和质量受限。

Method: 将语料库综合为三层维基（文档概览、跨文档主题页、来源记录），训练代理通过多轮工具调用导航；采用同策略强化学习，奖励函数结合答案正确性、检索质量和轨迹效率。

Result: 在ViDoRe-V3、FinanceBench及多个记忆基准上，WikiResearcher-9B显著优于同规模基线，并超过或匹配更大模型。

Conclusion: 在结构化语料库上进行学习导航是优于平面检索的检索增强生成方案。

中文摘要：平面检索增强生成将语料库视为块的集合，丢弃文档层次结构和跨文档结构。我们引入了SearchWiki，一个框架，将语料库综合成层次化的、类型化的、可导航的维基，并训练一个代理WikiResearcher-9B，通过多轮工具使用来检索信息。该维基将知识组织为三层——文档概览、跨文档主题页面和页面级来源记录；当初始查找未命中时，实现渐进式检索细化。我们使用多组件奖励函数优化代理的导航策略，该函数平衡答案正确性、检索质量和轨迹效率，采用同策略强化学习。在ViDoRe-V3（8个领域）、FinanceBench以及记忆基准（LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-v2）上的评估表明，WikiResearcher-9B（我们经过RL调优的Qwen 9B模型）显著优于同规模的未训练基线，并超过或匹配更大的外部模型。SearchWiki与WikiResearcher-9B配对表明，在结构化语料库上的学习导航是平面检索的优越替代方案。

Original abstract: Flat retrieval-augmented generation treats a corpus as a bag of chunks, discarding document hierarchy and cross document structure. We introduce SearchWiki, a harness framework that synthesizes a corpus into a hierarchical, typed, navigable wiki and trains an agent, WikiResearcher-9B, to retrieve information through multi-turn tool use. The wiki organizes knowledge into three layers - document overviews, cross- document topic pages, and page-level source records; enabling progressive refinement of retrieval when initial lookup misses. We optimize the agent's navigation policy with on-policy reinforcement learning with a multi-component reward function balancing answer correctness, retrieval quality and trajectory efficiency. Evaluation on ViDoRe-V3 (8 domains), FinanceBench, and memory benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval, PersonaMem-v2) shows that WikiResearcher- 9B which is our RL-tuned Qwen 9B model, significantly outperforms same-size untrained baselines and exceeds or matches larger external models. SearchWiki paired with WikiResearcher-9B demonstrates that learned navigation over structured corpora is a superior alternative to flat retrieval.

</details>


### [48] [Review Before Trust: Source-Grounded Integrity Gates for AI-Assisted Personal Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29965)
*Nora Girda,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出证据门控的信任提升机制，用确定性监控验证LLM输出是否被源文档支持，可推广到代码agent的claims验证。

TL;DR: 提出一种证据门控的信任提升模型，确保大型语言模型生成的结构化医疗数据在通过确定性监控验证前不被永久采用，从而维护纵向健康记录的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的结构化医疗数据可能缺乏源文档支持，持久化未经验证的数据会污染纵向健康记录，影响后续医疗决策。

Method: 设计基于证据门控的信任提升模型：生成的数据保持临时状态，由确定性监控器验证唯一引文、同行字段和来源信息；拒绝无证据或模糊证据的候选，并保留供人工审查；在Medical DataCloud中实现。

Result: 全部22项测试通过；重放实验显示，97个数值候选中，模式验证接受97个，早期证据检查接受94个，强化策略仅接纳72个，25个转入人工审查。

Conclusion: 该模型在技术上能有效阻止生成内容未经充分证据就进入纵向健康记录并影响后续使用，保障系统完整性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型可以将医学文档转换为结构化数据，但看似合理的输出可能仍缺乏源文档支持。将此类输出持久化到纵向健康记录（一种随时间累积患者信息的记录）中，会产生完整性风险：未经验证的数据可能影响后续的摘要、趋势或预防性护理计算。我们引入了一种基于证据门控的信任提升模型，该模型在确定性监控器对照源文档验证之前，将生成的数据保持为临时状态。仅当源文档包含唯一支持性引文、相关字段出现在同一实验室行内，且保留了所需来源信息时，监控器才允许候选数据用于指定的下游用途。生成器不能批准自己的输出，缺失或模糊的证据会导致拒绝，被拒绝的候选数据仍可供人工审查，而不会被静默丢弃。我们在个人健康记录应用 Medical DataCloud 中实现了该模型，并通过自动化测试和已保存提取输出的重放对其进行了评估。所有22项一致性和突变测试均通过。重放覆盖了9份历史实验室PDF报告，包含102个手动标注的行。这些报告产生了97个数值候选：模式验证接受全部97个，早期的数据包级证据检查接受94个，而强化的引文和行级策略仅接纳72个，同时保留25个供审查。该研究评估的是系统完整性，而非临床正确性或临床安全性。结果表明，在纵向健康记录中，建立可执行的边界以防止生成式声明自行授权其被重用，在技术上是可行的。

Original abstract: Large language models can convert medical documents into structured data, but plausible output may still be unsupported by the source. Persisting such output in a longitudinal health record, a record that accumulates patient information over time, therefore creates an integrity risk: unverified data may influence later summaries, trends, or preventive-care computations. We introduce an evidence-gated trust-promotion model that keeps generated data provisional until a deterministic monitor verifies it against the source document. The monitor admits a candidate for a specified downstream use only when the source contains a unique supporting quotation, the relevant fields occur within the same laboratory row, and the required provenance is preserved. The generator cannot approve its own output, missing or ambiguous evidence causes refusal, and refused candidates remain available for human review rather than being silently discarded. We implement the model in Medical DataCloud, a personal health-record application, and evaluate it through automated tests and a replay of saved extraction outputs. All 22 conformance and mutation tests pass. The replay covers nine historical laboratory PDF reports containing 102 manually labelled rows. The reports produce 97 numeric candidates: schema validation accepts all 97, an earlier packet-level evidence check accepts 94, and the hardened quotation- and row-level policy admits 72 while retaining 25 for review. The study evaluates system integrity rather than clinical correctness or clinical safety. The results demonstrate the technical feasibility of an enforceable boundary that prevents generated claims from authorizing their own reuse in a longitudinal health record.

</details>


### [49] [EDGE: Engine for Deterministic Graph Evaluation through Conversation Simulation from Graph Structured DSL Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29971)
*Ram Kulathumani,Regunathan Radhakrishnan,Anupam Tripathi,Xiangbo Mao,Roshanak Omrani,Keshav Somani,Shwet Kamal Mishra,Shayna Lurya*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于图遍历和DSL配置系统评测agent行为确定性与一致性，对agent可靠性测试有直接借鉴价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AgentGraph的形式化评估方法，通过图遍历枚举行为空间并重放轨迹，定义确定性等指标，证明结构化节点转换可提升智能体确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排工作流日益复杂，现有方法缺乏系统性框架来衡量智能体的行为一致性和确定性，导致可靠性难以保证。

Method: 利用AgentGraph的DSL规划器，将推理表示为动态有向图；使用图遍历算法穷举对话路径形成评估集；重放轨迹比较输出与DSL规范；定义响应/轨迹确定性、结构依从性和语义一致性等指标。

Result: 实验表明，使用AgentGraph和LangGraph等显式结构节点转换的智能体，其确定性优于未受控转换的智能体。

Conclusion: 结构化节点转换有助于提升智能体的确定性，所提方法可有效量化智能体行为可靠性，为复杂智能体系统评估提供新途径。

中文摘要：随着智能体系统演变为复杂的多智能体编排工作流，系统性框架来衡量智能体的行为一致性和确定性的需求日益增长且至关重要。在本文中，我们引入了一种形式化评估方法，该方法基于AgentGraph——一种由领域特定语言驱动的规划器，通过动态可调的有向图来表示智能体推理。我们利用这种结构化形式，并采用图遍历算法穷举枚举对话路径，形成覆盖智能体完整行为空间的综合评估集。然后，我们系统地重放这些可复现轨迹，将观察到的输出和状态转换与预期的DSL规范进行比较。为了量化可靠性，我们定义了新颖的指标，用于衡量响应和轨迹确定性、结构一致性以及语义一致性，涵盖精确重放及其语言变体。我们系统的结果表明，使用AgentGraph和LangGraph等框架配置的、具有显式结构化节点转换的智能体，在确定性方面优于未配置受控转换的智能体。

Original abstract: As agentic systems evolve into complex multi agent orchestration workflows, there is a growing and critical need for systematic frameworks that measures an agent's behavioral consistency and determinism. In this paper, we introduce a formal evaluation methodology that is grounded in AgentGraph, a planner powered by a domain specific language that represents agent reasoning through a dynamically adjustable directed graph. We leverage this structural formalism and utilize graph traversal algorithms that exhaustively enumerate conversational paths, forming a comprehensive evaluation set that captures the agent's complete behavioral space. We then systematically replay these reproducible trajectories to compare observed outputs and state transitions against the intended DSL specification. To quantify reliability, we define novel metrics that measure response and trajectory determinism, structural adherence and semantic consistency across both exact replays and their linguistic variants. Our system's results demonstrate that agents configured using frameworks like AgentGraph and LangGraph with explicitly structured node transitions show superior determinism over agents that are not configured with controlled transitions.

</details>


### [50] [AutoCRAT: Within-trajectory Joint Control of Stochasticity and Compute for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29988)
*Hanjun Luo,Qiushi Liu,Jingya Zhang,Haihong Pang,Jiaheng Wen,Yifei Ma,Yu Yao,Chengxi Zhang,Hanrong Zhang,Yankai Chen,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: EMNLP 2026 Findings

Selection reason: 在推理轨迹中联合控制采样随机性与计算预算，属于推理效率控制方法，可迁移到agent的推理资源管理。

TL;DR: AutoCRAT 是一个解码端控制器，能在单个推理轨迹内联合调整采样随机性和推理预算，从而以更少的词元获得更高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时决策多采用静态统一策略，而自适应方法仅单独调整解码随机性或推理计算量，忽略了同一推理轨迹中二者的交互作用。

Method: AutoCRAT 作为冻结骨干网络的解码端控制器，仅利用解码信号，在离散动作空间中于语义边界处联合更新采样随机性和推理预算。

Result: 在 6 个基准上，AutoCRAT 平均减少 13.8%-52.7% 的推理词元，相对准确率比静态和自适应基线高 1.5%-4.5%，并展现出强跨骨干迁移性。

Conclusion: 轨迹内联合控制推理决策是提升 LLM 推理效率与性能的有效方向，AutoCRAT 验证了无需修改骨干网络即可实现自适应推理的可行性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）展现出强大的推理能力，这在很大程度上依赖于推理时的决策。然而，这些决策通常由静态的、一刀切的策略处理，限制了其对多样化任务和推理阶段的适应性。近期的自适应方法部分解决了这一局限，但它们主要孤立地调整解码随机性（模型如何探索）或推理计算量（模型推理多久），未能对单个推理轨迹内二者的交互进行建模。为应对这一挑战，我们转向轨迹内联合控制的视角，并将其实例化为 AutoCRAT——一个面向冻结骨干网络的解码端控制器。仅利用解码过程中可用的信号，AutoCRAT 在生成过程中联合调整采样随机性和推理预算。AutoCRAT 在离散动作空间上运行，并且仅在语义边界处更新控制决策，从而在保持对演化中推理过程响应的同时提升稳定性。在 6 个基准上的全面评估表明，AutoCRAT：（I）平均比推荐的静态配置少使用 13.8%-52.7% 的推理词元；（II）在相对准确率上比推荐的静态基线和自适应基线高出 1.5%-4.5%；（III）具有较强的跨骨干网络迁移性。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong reasoning performance, which depends critically on inference-time decisions. Yet these decisions are commonly handled by static, one-size-fits-all policies, limiting adaptation to diverse tasks and reasoning stages. Recent adaptive methods partially address this limitation, but they primarily adapt either decoding stochasticity (how the model explores) or reasoning compute (how long the model reasons) in isolation, leaving their interaction within a single reasoning trajectory unmodeled. To address this challenge, we shift toward a within-trajectory joint control view, and instantiate it in AutoCRAT, a decoder-side controller for frozen backbones. Using only signals available during decoding, AutoCRAT jointly adjusts sampling stochasticity and reasoning budget during generation. AutoCRAT operates over a discrete action space and updates control decisions only at semantic boundaries, improving stability while remaining responsive to the evolving reasoning process. Comprehensive evaluation across 6 benchmarks demonstrates that AutoCRAT (I) uses 13.8-52.7% fewer inference tokens on average than recommended static configurations, (II) surpasses recommended static and adaptive baselines by 1.5-4.5% in relative accuracy, and (III) enjoys strong cross-backbone transferability.

</details>


### [51] [Interpreting and Steering for Safe and Correct Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30025)
*Hao Yan,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to the EMNLP 2026 Main Conference

Selection reason: 对LLM代码生成进行机制解释并提出双重steering降低漏洞率、提高功能正确性，直接面向代码agent的安全与正确性。

TL;DR: 提出DuoSteer，一种通过同时引导安全性和代码正确性注意力头来减少LLM生成代码漏洞的双重引导方法，在多个模型上显著降低漏洞率并提升功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的代码常含漏洞，但内部机制尚不明确，缺乏可操作的引导策略。

Method: 构建CodeSec-Pairs对比数据集，定位与代码安全相关的层和注意力头，提出DuoSteer双重引导策略，同时施加安全性和代码正确性引导。

Result: 在五种漏洞类型上，DuoSteer平均降低漏洞率26.9%，提升功能正确性7.5%，优于其他引导、提示和微调基线，并在另一模型上验证。

Conclusion: 通过机制解释和双重引导可有效促进安全代码生成，且具有跨模型泛化能力。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）经常生成包含漏洞的源代码，但很少有工作研究其内部机制如何区分安全生成与易受攻击生成。在这项工作中，我们系统地进行了LLM的机制解释，旨在理解代码安全性与漏洞如何由LM中的组件表示或驱动，并将这些见解转化为可操作的引导策略以鼓励更安全的代码生成。为此，我们引入了CodeSec-Pairs，一个包含9,342个Python安全与易受攻击对比代码对的数据集，从Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中采样。利用该数据集，我们探索了定位与代码安全性相关的层和注意力头的方法，并进一步尝试了不同的推理时漏洞减少的引导策略。特别地，我们提出了DuoSteer，一种双重引导方法，同时将安全性和代码正确性引导应用于注意力头。在五种漏洞类型的实验中，DuoSteer平均实现了-26.9%的漏洞率降低和+7.5%的功能正确性提升，不仅优于其他引导变体，也优于提示和监督微调基线。该优势也在Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上复制，使用了从该模型采样的另外2,500个对比对。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate source code containing vulnerabilities, yet little work studies the internal mechanisms that distinguish safe from vulnerable generation in them. In this work, we systematically perform a mechanistic interpretation of LLMs, aiming at both understanding how code safety-vs-vulnerability is represented or driven by components in an LM and turning the insights into actionable steering strategies to encourage safer code generation. To this end, we introduce CodeSec-Pairs, a dataset of 9,342 Python safe-and-vulnerable contrastive code pairs, sampled from Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Utilizing the dataset, we explore approaches to localize layers and attention heads that relate to code safety, and further experiment with different steering strategies for inference-time vulnerability reduction. In particular, we propose DuoSteer, a double-steering approach that simultaneously applies safety and code-correctness steering to attention heads. In experiments over five vulnerability types, DuoSteer leads to an average of -26.9% vulnerability rate reduction and +7.5% functional correctness improvement, which outperforms not only other steering variants but also prompting and supervised fine-tuning baselines. The advantage also replicates on Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct with another 2,500 contrastive pairs sampled from that model.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Uncertainty: Multi-Solver Disagreement Rewards for Self-Evolving Reasoning Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30035)
*Vinoth Selvendran,Zhanming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Proceedings of the 35th ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management (CIKM '26), November 07--11, 2026, Rome, Italy

DOI: [10.1145/3799682.3840051](https://doi.org/10.1145/3799682.3840051)

Selection reason: 用多求解器分歧奖励构建自适应课程，可视为agent自我进化/训练中的奖励设计，对自改进框架有启发。

TL;DR: 提出用多求解器分歧奖励替代单模型采样不确定性作为挑战者的奖励，通过异构集成间的香农熵捕捉真正的难题，提升自进化推理框架的课程质量，实验显示在竞赛数学基准上平均提升1.34分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化推理框架使用单一求解器的采样不确定性作为挑战者奖励，导致当求解器在特定问题分布上变得自信时，采样答案趋同、奖励归零，挑战者失去学习信号；且无法区分真正困难的问题与仅与求解器偏差一致的问题，造成根本性瓶颈。

Method: 设计一个多求解器分歧奖励，使用在模型容量和采样温度上不同的异构集成；对集成中每个问题的多个答案计算归一化香农熵，奖励产生冲突解决方案的问题，将难度定义为模型间分歧而非模型内采样方差。该奖励作为即插即用的替代，不修改框架或增加数据。

Result: 使用Qwen3-4B进行实验，在分歧挑战者问题上训练的求解器在MATH-500、AMC和Olympiad等竞赛数学基准上平均提升+1.34分，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 多求解器分歧是一种互补且可扩展的奖励信号，能有效改进自博弈推理系统的课程生成，使求解器发展出跨问题类型的稳健推理策略。

中文摘要：自进化推理框架训练一个挑战者（Challenger）来生成问题，以暴露求解器（Solver）的弱点，从而在没有人类数据的情况下创建自适应课程。然而，现有方法使用单个求解器的采样不确定性作为挑战者的奖励。这造成了一个根本性的瓶颈：随着求解器在挑战者的问题分布上变得更加自信，所有采样答案趋同一致，奖励归零，使挑战者失去学习信号。关键在于，这种单一模型奖励无法区分真正简单的问题与仅仅迎合单个求解器学习偏差的问题。我们提出了一种使用异构集成（在模型容量和采样温度上有所不同）的多求解器分歧奖励。对集成中每个问题的多数答案计算归一化香农熵，明确奖励求解器产生冲突解决方案的问题——将难度捕获为模型间分歧，而非模型内采样方差。这种更丰富的梯度使挑战者能够发现针对真实能力边界的问题，生成一个课程，迫使下游求解器发展出跨问题类型泛化的稳健推理策略。我们的方法是一个即插即用的奖励函数替换，无需框架修改或额外数据。使用Qwen3-4B的实验表明，在分歧挑战者问题上训练的求解器在竞赛数学基准（MATH-500、AMC、奥林匹克）上平均提高+1.34分，表明多求解器分歧为自博弈推理系统中的课程生成提供了互补且可扩展的信号。

Original abstract: Self-evolving reasoning frameworks train a Challenger to generate questions exposing a Solver's weaknesses, creating adaptive curricula without human data. However, existing approaches use a single solver's sampling uncertainty as the Challenger's reward. This creates a fundamental bottleneck: as the solver grows confident on the Challenger's question distribution, all sampled answers converge identically, collapsing the reward to zero and starving the Challenger of learning signal. Critically, this single-model reward cannot distinguish genuinely easy questions from those that merely align with one solver's learned biases. We propose a multi-solver disagreement reward using a heterogeneous ensemble varying in model capacity and sampling temperature. A normalized Shannon entropy over the ensemble's per-question plurality answers explicitly rewards questions where solvers produce conflicting solutions---capturing difficulty as inter-model divergence rather than intra-model sampling variance. This richer gradient enables the Challenger to discover questions targeting true capability boundaries, producing a curriculum that forces downstream Solvers to develop robust reasoning strategies generalizing across problem types. Our approach is a drop-in reward function replacement requiring no framework modifications or additional data. Experiments with Qwen3-4B show that Solvers trained on disagreement-Challenger questions achieve +1.34 points average improvement on competition-math benchmarks (MATH-500, AMC, Olympiad), suggesting that multi-solver disagreement provides a complementary and scalable signal for curriculum generation in self-play reasoning systems.

</details>


### [53] [Balance of Benchmarks: Semantic Density Reweighting for Benchmark Multiplicity and Task-Conditioned Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30044)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 针对benchmark列表密度进行语义重加权并支持任务条件化评估，对agent与代码优化benchmark设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 引入基准平衡（BoB）方法，通过逆密度语义加权和残差场实现任务条件化预测与对基准多重性的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准比较采用相等权重，导致密集发表的基准隐式主导评估；需要显式控制基准列表组成。

Method: BoB嵌入基准描述，分配逆密度权重，并在共同潜在尺度上利用残差场进行任务查询条件化排名。

Result: 在586个模型和14个基准上，剖面相关性0.462 vs 0.049；加入重复副本后Kendall tau 0.995 vs 0.936。

Conclusion: BoB使基准列表组成成为可控的测量设计部分，支持任务感知与多重性鲁棒的评估。

中文摘要：语言模型通常通过在基准列表上以相等权重平均得分来进行比较。这些列表通过发表而增长，但并非出于明确的测量设计，因此相等权重将已发表基准的密度转化为隐式能力权重：密集基准测试的区域会被重复计数。我们引入了基准平衡（BoB），它嵌入基准描述并为每个基准分配一个逆密度语义权重。相邻条目在公开的密度尺度上共享聚合影响力。在将异质分数折算到共同潜在尺度后，残差场利用相同的几何结构根据任务查询来调节模型排名。这两个组件分别发挥不同的实证作用。在包含586个模型和14个基准的快照上，BoB能预测哪些模型在保留任务上超出其一般能力而异常强大，其剖面相关性达到0.462，而相等权重下为0.049。它还限制了密集重复基准对聚合结果的影响。在依次添加每个基准的四份副本后，所得排名保持Kendall tau为0.995，而相等权重下为0.936。因此，残差场提供任务条件化的预测，而逆密度权重则提供对基准多重性的鲁棒性。二者结合，将基准列表组成从评估套件的偶然属性转变为测量设计中明确、可控的部分，为任务感知和多重性鲁棒的模型评估提供了原则性基础。

Original abstract: Language models are commonly compared by averaging scores across a benchmark list with equal weight. Such lists grow through publication outside an explicit measurement design, so equal weighting turns the density of published benchmarks into an implicit capability weight: densely benchmarked regions count repeatedly. We introduce Balance of Benchmarks (BoB), which embeds benchmark descriptions and assigns each benchmark an inverse-density semantic weight. Nearby entries share aggregate influence at a disclosed density scale. After equating heterogeneous scores onto a common latent scale, a residual field uses the same geometry to condition model rankings on a task query. The two components serve distinct empirical roles. On a snapshot of 586 models and 14 benchmarks, BoB predicts which models are unusually strong on a held-out task beyond their general ability, reaching a profile correlation of 0.462 compared with 0.049 under equal weighting. It also limits the influence of densely repeated benchmarks on the aggregate. After adding four copies of each benchmark in turn, the resulting rankings retain a Kendall tau of 0.995, compared with 0.936 under equal weighting. The residual field therefore provides task-conditioned prediction, and inverse-density weighting provides robustness to benchmark multiplicity. Together, they turn benchmark-list composition from an incidental property of evaluation suites into an explicit, controllable part of measurement design, providing a principled foundation for task-aware and multiplicity-robust model evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [Can LLM Agents Discover? Evaluating Creativity on ML Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30047)
*Shitanshu Bhushan,Yunxiang Zhang,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: COLM 2026

Selection reason: 多轮LLM agent在ML工程任务上的创造力评测框架，结合LLM-as-Judge与人类判断，对agent能力评估有方法贡献。

TL;DR: 提出一个评估多轮LLM研究智能体创造力的框架，从P-创造力、H-创造力和实用性三个维度分析，发现智能体在探索转向利用时P-创造力下降，H-创造力高于人类但性能较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AI系统是否具备创造力（既新颖又有用）仍是一个未解决问题，现有评估方法缺乏系统性框架，特别是在多轮LLM研究智能体上的应用。

Method: 使用ML工程任务作为测试平台，定义三个创造力维度（P-创造性、H-创造力、实用性），开发LLM-as-a-Judge流水线，并在10个Kaggle式任务上评估AIDE和AIRA-Dojo两种智能体框架，与人类创造力判断进行相关性验证。

Result: 所有智能体在从探索转向利用时P-创造力下降；LLM比获奖人类展现更高H-创造力但性能更低；智能体能探索新颖区域但无法将新颖性转化为性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前AI研究智能体缺乏将创造性探索转化为实际任务性能改进的能力，需要进一步研究以桥接新颖性与实用性之间的差距。

中文摘要：近期的人工智能系统承诺实现自主科学发现，声称能够发现算法并生成研究论文，然而理解它们是否展现出创造力——即产生既新颖又有用的解决方案的能力——仍然是一个未解的问题。我们提出了一个框架，以机器学习工程任务为测试平台，从三个维度评估多轮大语言模型（LLM）研究智能体的创造力：P-创造力（心理新颖性：相对于智能体在单次运行中自身先前的解决方案而言的新颖性）、H-创造力（历史新颖性：相对于人类解决方案语料库而言的新颖性）以及实用性（任务性能）。我们在来自MLE-Bench的10个Kaggle式机器学习任务上评估了两个智能体框架AIDE和AIRA-Dojo，开发了一个“LLM作为评判者”的流水线，并验证了其与人类创造力判断的强相关性，为大规模P-创造力评估提供了可靠的自动化指标。将该流水线应用于智能体轨迹，我们发现：（1）所有智能体在从探索转向利用时，P-创造力均下降；（2）LLM比获奖人类展现出更高的H-创造力，但取得了更低的性能。我们的研究结果表明，当前的智能体能够探索解决方案空间中的新颖区域，但缺乏将这种新颖性转化为改进任务性能的能力。

Original abstract: Recent AI systems promise autonomous scientific discovery, claiming to discover algorithms and produce research papers, yet understanding whether they exhibit creativity, the capacity to produce solutions that are both novel and useful, remains an open question. We present a framework for evaluating multi-turn LLM research agents' creativity using ML engineering tasks as a testbed, through three dimensions: P-Creativity (psychological novelty: novel relative to the agent's own prior solutions within a run), H-Creativity (historical novelty: novel relative to the corpus of human solutions), and Usefulness (task performance). Evaluating two agent frameworks, AIDE and AIRA-Dojo, on 10 Kaggle-style machine learning tasks from MLE-Bench, we develop an LLM-as-a-Judge pipeline and verify its strong correlation with human creativity judgments, providing a reliable automated metric for P-Creativity evaluation at scale. Applying this pipeline to agent trajectories, we find: (1) all agents exhibit declining P-Creativity as they transition from exploration to exploitation; (2) LLMs exhibit greater H-Creativity than medal-winning humans, yet achieve lower performance. Our findings reveal that current agents can explore novel regions of the solution space but lack the capacity to convert this novelty into improved task performance.

</details>


### [55] [Spec2Twin-Chain: Orchestrating Bi-Level Optimization with LLMs for Blockchain Digital Twin Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30050)
*Haoting Zhang,Haoxian Chen,Jiayuan Sheng,Donglin Zhan,Zeyu Zheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM与仿真优化器构成双层优化框架，并通过全局档案反馈改进提案，可迁移到代码优化中的探索-验证循环。

TL;DR: Spec2Twin-Chain通过双层优化自动化区块链数字孪生构建，利用大语言模型和模拟优化器，实现行为准确的孪生并支持下游决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链数字孪生构建依赖特定应用建模，难以跨系统和下游决策重用，需要自动化方法。

Method: 提出Spec2Twin-Chain框架，将构建过程建模为双层优化：上层LLM基于规范和反馈提出/修订架构，下层模拟优化器校准参数，并利用全局档案指导迭代。

Result: 实验表明该框架能构建行为准确的孪生，通过迭代反馈改进初始设计，并重用校准的孪生支持下游策略优化和更新。

Conclusion: 该方法可自动化区块链数字孪生构建，提高可重用性，为下游决策提供可靠支持。

中文摘要：构建区块链数字孪生很大程度上需要将领域知识和特定系统描述转化为模拟器架构，根据行为证据校准其参数，并验证所构建的孪生。这些步骤通常通过特定于应用的建模工作来完成，这些工作难以跨系统和下游决策问题重用。我们考虑通过Spec2Twin-Chain自动化这一过程，该框架将区块链数字孪生构建表述为一个双层优化问题。在上层，大语言模型利用系统规范、行为证据和来自评估设计的反馈来提出和修订结构上合理的架构。在下层，基于模拟的优化器在明确目标和护栏约束下校准架构条件参数。两层迭代。下层的评估候选保留在全局档案中，用于指导上层的后续提案。我们进行了受控实验，涉及孪生校准、反馈驱动恢复、压力分析、下游策略优化和策略更新。结果表明，该框架能够构建行为准确的孪生，通过迭代反馈改进初始设计，并重用校准的孪生以支持下游决策。

Original abstract: Building a blockchain digital twin largely requires translating domain knowledge and specific system descriptions into a simulator architecture, calibrating its parameters against behavioral evidence, and validating the constructed twin. These steps are commonly performed through application-specific modeling efforts that can be difficult to reuse across systems and downstream decision problems. We consider automating this process through Spec2Twin-Chain, a framework that formulates blockchain digital-twin construction as a bi-level optimization problem. At the upper level, a large language model proposes and revises structurally admissible architectures using system specifications, behavioral evidence, and feedback from evaluated designs. At the lower level, a simulation-based optimizer calibrates the architecture-conditioned parameters under explicit objectives and guardrail constraints. The two levels iterate. The evaluated candidates at lower levels are retained in a global archive and used to guide subsequent proposals at upper levels. We conduct controlled experiments involving twin calibration, feedback-driven recovery, stress analysis, downstream policy optimization, and policy updating. The results demonstrate that the framework can construct behaviorally accurate twins, improve initial designs through iterative feedback, and reuse calibrated twins to support downstream decisions.

</details>


### [56] [Mitigating Over-Optimization in PRM-Guided Search in Mathematical Reasoning by Optimizing the Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30051)
*Taejong Joo,Diego Klabjan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 针对PRM引导搜索的过度优化问题提出训练无关鲁棒方法，测试时搜索改进可迁移至编程agent的推理。

TL;DR: 提出极大极小PRM引导搜索，一种无需训练的鲁棒过程监督方法，通过将搜索形式化为鲁棒优化问题来缓解PRM分数噪声导致的过度优化，平均提升17-35%，在16个设置中14个优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM引导的搜索可能过度优化不完美的过程奖励，导致剪除可行轨迹而扩展虚假轨迹，原因是随着推理深度增加，非可行前缀更容易获得虚假高分（极值效应）。

Method: 将PRM引导搜索形式化为极大极小鲁棒优化问题，在合理奖励扰动下寻找最优策略，从而无需训练或在线适应即可降低对PRM异常值的敏感性。

Result: 在16个设置中的14个中，极大极小搜索平均提升PRM引导搜索17-35%，并优于结果级和步骤级基线。

Conclusion: 极大极小PRM引导搜索是一种有效的鲁棒过程监督方法，能够缓解验证器噪声带来的过度优化问题，无需额外训练即可显著提升搜索性能。

中文摘要：过程奖励模型（PRMs）为基于搜索的推理提供密集的逐步级指导，使得推理时的计算能够分配给有希望的局部解。然而，近期证据表明，PRM引导的搜索可能过度优化不完美的过程奖励，剪除可行的轨迹，同时扩展虚假的轨迹。在这项工作中，我们从理论上证明，直接利用PRM分数容易受到验证器噪声的影响，这种噪声通过一种极值效应显现：随着推理深度的增加，不可行的前缀更有可能获得虚假的高分。因此，我们将PRM引导的搜索表述为一个在合理奖励扰动上的鲁棒优化问题，称为极大极小PRM引导搜索，从而产生一种无需训练的鲁棒过程监督方法，在逐步分数存在噪声时保留有希望的备选方案。极大极小PRM引导搜索通过减少对过度优化的PRM异常值的敏感性来缓解这种失败模式。无需微调或在线适应，极大极小搜索在平均上一致地将PRM引导搜索提升了17-35%，在16个设置中的14个中优于结果级和步骤级基线。我们的源代码可在 https://github.com/tjoo512/maximin-search 获取。

Original abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) provide dense step-level guidance for search-based reasoning, enabling inference-time compute to be allocated toward promising partial solutions. However, recent evidence suggests that PRM-guided search can over-optimize imperfect process rewards, pruning viable trajectories while expanding spurious ones. In this work, we theoretically show that directly leveraging PRM score is vulnerable to verifier noise through an extreme-value effect: non-viable prefixes become more likely to receive spuriously high scores as reasoning depth increase. Therefore, we formulate the PRM-guided search as a robust optimization problem over plausible reward perturbations, termed maximin PRM-guided search, leading to a training-free robust process supervision method that preserves promising alternatives when step-level scores are noisy. Maximin PRM-guided search mitigates this failure mode by reducing sensitivity to over-optimized PRM outliers. Without fine-tuning or online adaptation, maximin search consistently improves the PRM-guided search by 17-35\% on average, outperforming outcome- and step-level baselines in 14 out of 16 settings. Our source code is available at https://github.com/tjoo512/maximin-search.

</details>


### [57] [VERA: Authority-Preserving Edge Revocation for Federated AI-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30091)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多agent工作流的权限撤销与一致性验证，涉及agent基础设施可靠性与安全控制。

TL;DR: 提出VERA，一种可验证的边撤销契约，用于智能体运行时委派图中的权限一致性，解决了树级联过度撤销和部署者级联撤销不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架的撤销API仍基于令牌或子树失效，无法准确处理委派图中共享智能体的权限一致性。当一条委派被撤回时，需要区分真正失去权限的智能体和仍然独立授权的智能体，否则会过度或不足撤销。

Method: VERA通过智能体运行时适配器发出签名证据，基于析取权限定义精确的撤销目标T_intent(e,G) = reach(G) \ reach(G \ {e})，并作为可验证的撤销契约来检测树级联过度撤销和部署者级联撤销不足的问题。

Result: 在LangGraph框架实验中（20次重复，500个追踪，2,000个签名决策），VERA在500/500目标证明中保留所有320个替代父共享智能体案例，拒绝未经授权的签名者和遗漏攻击；基线重放（1,900个目标）无法表达该行为。在A2A、AutoGen和CrewAI工件上也验证了模式可移植性，九个追踪（含五个可执行Cregned委派事件）通过检查。

Conclusion: VERA提供了一种精确、可验证的智能体委派撤销方案，适用于多种框架，能够避免现有撤销机制中的过度/不足撤销问题。

中文摘要：现代智能体框架将规划器、工具智能体、远程服务和共享专家组合成运行时委派图，但它们的撤销API仍然类似于令牌或子树失效。当一个委派被撤回时，运行时必须知道哪些智能体失去权限，而独立授权的智能体继续工作。我们研究这个权限一致性问题，并引入VERA（Verifiable Edge Revocation for Agents，可验证的智能体边撤销），一种由智能体运行时适配器以签名证据形式发出的可验证检查撤销契约和API。在分离权限下，撤销边e恰好使T_intent(e,G) = reach(G) \ reach(G \ {e})失效，这些智能体的每个授权根路径都使用了e。作为契约使用时，该目标暴露了两种运行时失败：树级联会过度撤销共享智能体，而部署者范围的级联会不足撤销跨域后代。在LangGraph框架中，重复20次的单元产生500个编译框架追踪和2,000个有效的签名委派决策；13/25个单元包含运行时多父共享，8/25个包含跨部署者场景。500/500个目标证明保留了树级联撤销的所有320个替代父共享智能体案例，并拒绝未经授权的签名者和遗漏攻击。基线重放超过1,900个持有者/节点和树风格目标无法表达此行为。我们进一步在A2A、AutoGen和CrewAI工件上验证了模式可移植性：九个追踪，包括五个可执行的Cregned委派事件，通过了模式和签名检查。

Original abstract: Modern agent frameworks compose planners, tool agents, remote services, and shared specialists into runtime delegation graphs, but their revocation APIs still resemble token or subtree invalidation. When one delegation is withdrawn, the runtime must know which agents lose authority while independently authorized agents keep working. We study this authority consistency problem and introduce VERA (Verifiable Edge Revocation for Agents), a verifier-checkable revocation contract and API emitted by agent-runtime adapters as signed evidence. Under disjunctive authority, revoking edge e invalidates exactly T_intent(e,G) = reach(G) \ reach(G \ {e}), the agents whose every authorizing root path used e. Used as a contract, this target exposes two runtime failures: tree cascades over-revoke shared agents, while deployer-scoped cascades under-revoke cross-domain descendants. In a LangGraph framework-replt cells repeated 20 times yield 500compiled-framework traces and 2,000 valid signed delegation decisions; 13/25 cells contain runtime multi-parsharing and 8/25 contain cross-deployer shies 500/500 target proofs, preserves all320 alternate-parent shared-agent cases that tree cascade revokes, and rejects unauthorized signers and omission attacks. Baseline replay over 1,9that holder/node and tree-style targetscannot express this behavior. We further validate schema portability on A2A, AutoGen, and CrewAI artifacts: nine traces, including five executable Cregned delegation events that pass schema and signature checks.

</details>


### [58] [FaVOR: LLM-Based Agentic Framework for Factor Mining via Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30192)
*Hyeonjin Kim,Minseok Kim,Seunghyeon Jung,Sujin Pyo,Huisu Jang,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM多agent因子挖掘框架，用经验验证与假设一致性循环，通用验证方法可迁移。

TL;DR: FaVOR通过三阶段一致性循环将因子挖掘与经济学假设对齐，在2025年CSI 500和S&P 500上优于基线且跨制度稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收益导向的自动化因子挖掘忽视因子是否表达其经济假设，导致数学形式与经济含义不一致，产生虚假相关并在制度转变中失效。

Method: FaVOR框架用假设层面证据取代收益结果，通过分解、验证、整合三阶段循环，强制数学形式与经济原理保持一致。

Result: 在2025年CSI 500和S&P 500上，FaVOR优于现有基线，并在不同市场制度下保持有效。

Conclusion: 基于假设的因子发现能产生构造上可解释、制度稳健且经济上忠实的信号。

中文摘要：传统金融依赖专家通过基于经济原理的原则性过程手工构建因子。近期基于LLM的多智能体系统自动化了这一过程，将因子挖掘规模扩展到远超人工努力的程度。然而，这些自动化方法直接优化收益，很少检查生成的因子是否仍然表达了激发它的经济假设。我们将这种数学形式与经济含义之间的不一致识别为收益导向自动化的结构性失败模式。由此产生的因子模糊了真实信号与虚假相关性之间的界限，并在制度转变中失效。我们提出FaVOR（基于可观测推理的因子验证），一个智能体框架，将因子挖掘重构为围绕假设层面证据而非收益结果。取代标准的假设到公式的跳跃，FaVOR强制执行一个三阶段一致性循环，将数学形式与经济原理贯穿始终。（1）分解将广泛的经济假设拆分为独立的可观测条件。（2）验证检查每个因子是否反映其预期条件。（3）整合将它们合并为一个结构仍可解释的复合因子。在2025年的CSI 500和S&P 500上，FaVOR优于现有基线，并在不同制度下保持有效。FaVOR表明，基于假设的因子发现产生的信号在构造上可解释、对制度稳健且经济上忠实。代码可在https://github.com/damilab/FaVOR获取。

Original abstract: Traditional finance relies on experts to hand-craft factors through a principled process grounded in economic rationale. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have automated this process, scaling factor mining far beyond manual effort. However, these automated approaches optimize directly for returns and rarely check whether a generated factor still expresses the economic hypothesis that motivated it. We identify this inconsistency between mathematical form and economic meaning as a structural failure mode of return-oriented automation. The resulting factors blur the line between real signals and spurious correlations and break down across regime shifts. We propose FaVOR (Factor Validation through Observable Reasoning), an agentic framework that restructures factor mining around hypothesis-level evidence rather than return outcomes. In place of the standard hypothesis-to-formula leap, FaVOR enforces a three-stage consistency loop tying mathematical form to economic rationale throughout. (1) Decomposition splits a broad economic hypothesis into independent observable conditions. (2) Validation checks whether each factor reflects its intended condition. (3) Integration merges them into a composite whose structure remains interpretable. On the CSI 500 and S&P 500 in 2025, FaVOR outperforms existing baselines while remaining effective across regimes. FaVOR shows that hypothesis-grounded factor discovery produces signals that are interpretable by construction, regime-robust, and economically faithful. The code is available at https://github.com/damilab/FaVOR.

</details>


### [59] [Generating Workflow DAGs from Natural Language with Non-Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30250)
*Anand Iyer,Bhanu Khetharpal,Srinivas Upadhya,Ramkumar Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用神经符号分解将自然语言转为工作流DAG，结构化生成方法对代码生成有启发。

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号分解方法，将自然语言路由规则转换为工作流DAG，通过确定性编译器结合紧凑中间表示，降低了对大模型推理能力的要求，在成本更低的情况下达到接近推理模型的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业联络中心需要将业务管理员编写的自然语言路由规则转换为可执行的工作流图（DAG）。现有的扩展推理模型成本高昂，而普通模型在生成复杂DAG时存在发射密度瓶颈：节点选择准确但属性和布尔分组配置易出错。目标是利用低成本模型实现生产级质量，同时减少提示词消耗。

Method: 采用神经符号分解：将组合图构建从模型中转移到确定性编译器，该编译器由紧凑的中间表示驱动；前端使用学习型注册表选择机制，聚焦于相关词汇的生成。模型只需生成中间表示，而非直接输出完整图。

Result: 在四个模型上，系统达到约89% LLM评判有效性、约90%精确匹配条件准确率、99-100%有效JSON，且提示词token约为单体提示的一半。在GPT-5.3-chat上，评判有效性提升24个百分点，统计上等同于推理模型的开箱即用质量，但仍有约8个百分点的前沿差距。

Conclusion: 神经符号分解是降低复杂结构化生成成本的有效路径，为生产级工作流图生成提供了可行方案，并给出了部署路径和可迁移经验，适用于其他结构化生成任务。
